📘 國考教材首頁/ 共同科目/英文題庫
📖 字彙 230 · 文法 200 · 克漏字 12 篇 · 閱讀測驗 28 篇 · 共 620 題

英文題庫（含全文中譯）

20 篇克漏字與閱讀測驗，全部附完整繁體中文翻譯，
讀不懂的地方可以直接對照，不必再查字典。

📌 使用說明

步驟做法
1先限時作答——字彙、文法每題 40 秒；克漏字一篇 4 分鐘；閱讀一篇 5 分鐘
2再展開詳解，對照中譯找出自己是「單字不懂」還是「句構看不懂」
3把不認識的字抄進自己的單字本——這些字在考場再出現的機率最高
4一週後重做一次錯題（不看中譯）
⚠️ 中譯的正確用法 中譯是「檢討工具」，不是「閱讀工具」。
作答時絕對不要先看中譯——那會讓你以為自己看懂了，實際上考場沒有中譯可看。
正確順序：作答 → 對答案 → 才看中譯找出問題在哪。

A. 字彙（1～40 題）

1
The government plans to ____ a new online system to simplify tax filing.
(A) implement　(B) imply　(C) impose　(D) improve
詳解
答案：(A) implement 實施、推行
中譯：政府計畫推行一套新的線上系統，以簡化報稅程序。

其他選項：(B) imply 暗示｜(C) impose 強加、課徵（impose a tax 課稅）｜(D) improve 改善
搭配：implement a policy / system / plan —— 公文英文極高頻。
2
Only citizens who meet certain criteria are ____ for the housing subsidy.
(A) capable　(B) eligible　(C) available　(D) reliable
詳解
答案：(B) eligible 有資格的
中譯：只有符合特定條件的民眾才具備申請住宅補貼的資格。

四個 -able 結尾字的區別：
eligible for/to V符合資格的（公文最常用）
capable of有能力做…的
available可取得的、有空的
reliable可靠的
記法：eligible 講的是資格，capable 講的是能力。
3
The data ____ exposed the personal information of over 100,000 residents.
(A) breach　(B) branch　(C) bridge　(D) brief
詳解
答案：(A) breach（資料）外洩、違反
中譯：這起資料外洩事件使十萬名以上居民的個人資料曝光。

資安必背字：data breach 資料外洩｜breach of contract 違約｜breach of duty 違反義務
其他選項：(B) branch 分支、分行｜(C) bridge 橋樑｜(D) brief 簡短的／簡報
4
Regular software updates help reduce system ____ to cyberattacks.
(A) durability　(B) vulnerability　(C) capability　(D) possibility
詳解
答案：(B) vulnerability 脆弱性、（資安）漏洞
中譯：定期更新軟體有助於降低系統面對網路攻擊時的脆弱性。

資安對照：vulnerability 漏洞｜threat 威脅｜risk 風險｜patch 修補程式
形容詞：vulnerable to + N（易受…傷害的）——常考搭配。
5
The committee will ____ the effectiveness of the program before extending it.
(A) evacuate　(B) evaluate　(C) elevate　(D) eliminate
詳解
答案：(B) evaluate 評估
中譯：委員會將先評估該計畫的成效，再決定是否延長。

四個易混字：(A) evacuate 疏散｜(C) elevate 提升｜(D) eliminate 消除
名詞形：evaluation 評估——與 assessment（評量）常互換。
6
Public agencies must ensure that their websites are ____ to people with disabilities.
(A) accessible　(B) acceptable　(C) accountable　(D) adaptable
詳解
答案：(A) accessible 可及的、無障礙的
中譯：公務機關必須確保其網站對身心障礙者而言是可及的（無障礙的）。

正是「無障礙網頁」的英文：web accessibility。
其他選項：(B) acceptable 可接受的｜(C) accountable 應負責的（accountable to the public 對公眾負責）｜(D) adaptable 適應力強的
7
The new regulation aims to ____ the administrative process and cut waiting time.
(A) streamline　(B) stream　(C) strengthen　(D) stretch
詳解
答案：(A) streamline 精簡（流程）
中譯：新法規旨在精簡行政流程並縮短等候時間。

行政英文高頻字：streamline the process / procedure
近義：simplify 簡化｜expedite 加速
8
Local governments received a ____ from the central government to build the facility.
(A) subsidy　(B) subsidiary　(C) substitute　(D) submission
詳解
答案：(A) subsidy 補助（款）
中譯：地方政府獲得中央政府的補助款以興建該設施。

其他選項：(B) subsidiary 子公司｜(C) substitute 替代品｜(D) submission 提交
相關：grant 補助金｜funding 經費｜allocate funds 撥款
9
All employees are required to ____ with the new security guidelines.
(A) comply　(B) complain　(C) complete　(D) compete
詳解
答案：(A) comply（with）遵守
中譯：所有員工均須遵守新的資安規範。

介系詞是考點：comply with（不是 comply to）
名詞形：compliance 法遵——資安與稽核文件的核心字。
10
The report ____ that air quality has improved over the past decade.
(A) indicates　(B) invites　(C) invents　(D) invests
詳解
答案：(A) indicates 顯示、指出
中譯：報告指出，過去十年間空氣品質已有改善。

「顯示」家族（閱讀測驗超高頻）：indicate、suggest、reveal、demonstrate、show
其中 suggest 在學術語境是「顯示」不是「建議」——這是常見的誤譯。
11
Taiwan is facing the challenges of an ____ society with a declining birth rate.
(A) aging　(B) aging up　(C) aged out　(D) age
詳解
答案：(A) aging 高齡化的
中譯：臺灣正面臨高齡化社會與出生率下降的挑戰。

必背詞組：an aging society 高齡社會｜declining birth rate 出生率下降｜life expectancy 平均餘命
這是國考英文最常出現的社會議題之一。
12
The policy has a ____ impact on small businesses in rural areas.
(A) significant　(B) signal　(C) signature　(D) signify
詳解
答案：(A) significant 顯著的、重大的
中譯：該政策對偏鄉小型企業有重大的影響。

詞性判斷即可解題：空格在 a ___ impact 之間，前有冠詞、後有名詞 → 必填形容詞。
(B) 名詞／動詞｜(C) 名詞｜(D) 動詞 → 全刪。

這題示範：不認識單字也能靠詞性拿分。
13
The agency will ____ a survey to understand residents' needs.
(A) conduct　(B) construct　(C) contact　(D) contract
詳解
答案：(A) conduct 執行、進行
中譯：該機關將進行一項調查，以了解居民的需求。

固定搭配：conduct a survey / study / research / investigation / audit
其他：(B) construct 建造｜(C) contact 聯絡｜(D) contract 契約／收縮
14
Digital transformation has ____ the way public services are delivered.
(A) transformed　(B) transferred　(C) transported　(D) translated
詳解
答案：(A) transformed 轉變、改造
中譯：數位轉型已徹底改變公共服務的提供方式。

trans- 家族：transform 轉變｜transfer 移轉｜transport 運輸｜translate 翻譯｜transmit 傳送
四個都是「跨越」，差別在後半段。
15
Officials must ____ the confidentiality of citizens' personal data.
(A) maintain　(B) contain　(C) obtain　(D) retain
詳解
答案：(A) maintain 維護、保持
中譯：公務人員必須維護民眾個人資料的機密性。

-tain 家族（極常混淆）：
maintain維持、維護
contain包含、抑制
obtain取得
retain保留
sustain維持、承受
相關名詞：confidentiality 機密性｜integrity 完整性｜availability 可用性——正是資安 CIA 三要素。
16
The proposal was rejected because it was not financially ____.
(A) feasible　(B) fashionable　(C) favorable　(D) familiar
詳解
答案：(A) feasible 可行的
中譯：該提案因財務上不可行而遭否決。

公文英文常見：feasibility study 可行性研究｜technically feasible 技術上可行
其他：(B) 時髦的｜(C) 有利的、贊成的｜(D) 熟悉的
17
Heavy rainfall has ____ in serious flooding in several districts.
(A) resulted　(B) caused　(C) led　(D) brought
詳解
答案：(A) resulted（in）導致
中譯：豪雨已導致數個行政區發生嚴重淹水。

介系詞是關鍵——空格後是 in：
result in導致（後接結果）
result from起因於（後接原因）
lead to導致（(C) 要配 to，不是 in）
cause / bring about直接接受詞，不用 in
result in / result from 的方向相反，是年年出現的考點。
18
The city has invested heavily in ____ such as roads, bridges, and public transit.
(A) infrastructure　(B) instrument　(C) institution　(D) instruction
詳解
答案：(A) infrastructure 基礎建設
中譯：該市已大量投資於道路、橋梁與大眾運輸等基礎建設。

解題線索：such as 後面舉的例子（roads, bridges, public transit）就是空格的定義。
「such as / for example / like」後面的例子，永遠是字彙題最好的線索。

延伸：critical infrastructure 關鍵基礎設施——資安法的核心概念。
19
Employees should be ____ of the risks of phishing emails.
(A) aware　(B) award　(C) awake　(D) away
詳解
答案：(A) aware（of）察覺、知悉
中譯：員工應了解釣魚郵件的風險。

固定搭配：be aware of + N｜raise awareness 提升意識
資安必背：security awareness training 資安認知訓練｜phishing email 釣魚郵件
20
The department is responsible for ____ resources to the most urgent projects.
(A) allocating　(B) alleviating　(C) allowing　(D) altering
詳解
答案：(A) allocating 分配
中譯：該部門負責將資源分配給最急迫的專案。

搭配：allocate resources / budget / funds to...
其他：(B) alleviate 緩解（alleviate poverty 減緩貧窮）｜(C) allow 允許｜(D) alter 改變
21
Despite the difficulties, the team managed to meet the ____.
(A) deadline　(B) headline　(C) outline　(D) guideline
詳解
答案：(A) deadline 期限
中譯：儘管困難重重，團隊仍設法趕上期限。

搭配：meet the deadline 趕上期限｜miss the deadline 錯過期限｜extend the deadline 展延
其他 -line：headline 標題｜outline 大綱｜guideline 準則
22
The government has taken steps to ____ the digital divide between urban and rural areas.
(A) bridge　(B) build　(C) break　(D) burn
詳解
答案：(A) bridge 弭平、跨越（作動詞）
中譯：政府已採取措施以弭平城鄉間的數位落差。

必背詞組：digital divide 數位落差｜bridge the gap / divide 弭平差距
同義說法：narrow the gap、close the gap。

注意：bridge 當動詞是「跨越、連接」的意思——名詞的印象反而會誤導。
23
Renewable energy sources are considered more ____ than fossil fuels.
(A) sustainable　(B) suitable　(C) sensible　(D) sociable
詳解
答案：(A) sustainable 永續的
中譯：再生能源被認為比化石燃料更具永續性。

環境類必背：renewable energy 再生能源｜fossil fuel 化石燃料｜sustainable development 永續發展｜carbon emissions 碳排放
24
The system automatically ____ unusual login attempts and alerts administrators.
(A) detects　(B) deletes　(C) delays　(D) delivers
詳解
答案：(A) detects 偵測
中譯：系統會自動偵測異常登入行為並通知管理者。

資安詞組：intrusion detection 入侵偵測｜detect anomalies 偵測異常｜alert 告警
解題線索：後半句 and alerts administrators（並通知管理者）→ 前面必然是「發現」了什麼。
25
The minister ____ the importance of protecting personal information.
(A) emphasized　(B) emptied　(C) employed　(D) enabled
詳解
答案：(A) emphasized 強調
中譯：部長強調保護個人資料的重要性。

「強調」家族：emphasize、stress、highlight、underscore——閱讀測驗的態度題常靠這些字判斷。
26
Applicants must submit the required documents ____ the end of this month.
(A) no later than　(B) no sooner than　(C) not less than　(D) no longer than
詳解
答案：(A) no later than 不遲於
中譯：申請人須於本月底前繳交所需文件。

公文英文必背片語：
no later than不遲於（…之前）
at the latest最遲
within 30 days30 日內
effective from自…生效
upon receipt of一經收到
27
The two agencies signed an agreement to ____ on cybersecurity issues.
(A) collaborate　(B) collapse　(C) collect　(D) collide
詳解
答案：(A) collaborate 合作
中譯：兩機關簽署協議，就資安議題進行合作。

搭配：collaborate with someone on something
近義：cooperate 合作｜coordinate 協調｜partnership 夥伴關係
其他：(B) 崩塌｜(C) 蒐集｜(D) 碰撞
28
Statistics show a ____ increase in online fraud cases last year.
(A) sharp　(B) shallow　(C) shy　(D) short
詳解
答案：(A) sharp 急遽的
中譯：統計顯示，去年網路詐騙案件數量急遽增加。

描述數據變化的形容詞（閱讀測驗高頻）：
幅度大幅度小穩定
sharp、dramatic、significant、substantial、steepslight、marginal、modest、gradualsteady、stable、constant
動詞：rise / increase / soar / surge（升）｜fall / decline / drop / plunge（降）
29
The proposal must be approved by the board ____ it can be implemented.
(A) before　(B) after　(C) unless　(D) despite
詳解
答案：(A) before 在…之前
中譯：該提案須經董事會核准，才能付諸實施。

邏輯判斷：核准 → 實施（先核准，後實施）。
(B) after 顛倒因果｜(C) unless 除非（語意不合）｜(D) despite 是介系詞，後面不能接子句 → 詞性即可刪。

常考陷阱：despite / in spite of 是介系詞（+ N）；although / though 是連接詞（+ 子句）。
30
The new law will take ____ on January 1 next year.
(A) effect　(B) affect　(C) effort　(D) effective
詳解
答案：(A) effect（名詞）
中譯：新法將於明年 1 月 1 日生效。

固定片語：take effect 生效｜come into effect 開始施行｜in effect 實際上／有效

effect vs affect（每年必考）：
effect名詞：影響、效果（have an effect on）
affect動詞：影響（affect the outcome）
記法：a-ffect 是 action（動詞），e-ffect 是 end result（名詞）。
31
Officials are working to restore public ____ after the scandal.
(A) trust　(B) trace　(C) trend　(D) trial
詳解
答案：(A) trust 信任
中譯：弊案之後，官員正努力重建公眾信任。

搭配：restore / rebuild / undermine / erode public trust
「侵蝕信任」用 erode 或 undermine——閱讀測驗常見。
32
Due to budget ____, the project has been postponed indefinitely.
(A) constraints　(B) contests　(C) contrasts　(D) contracts
詳解
答案：(A) constraints 限制
中譯：由於預算限制，該專案已無限期延後。

搭配：budget / time / resource constraints——公務情境高頻
近義：limitation、restriction
其他：(B) 競賽｜(C) 對比｜(D) 契約
33
The findings ____ that early intervention leads to better outcomes.
(A) suggest　(B) suspect　(C) suppose　(D) supply
詳解
答案：(A) suggest（此處為「顯示、指出」）
中譯：研究發現顯示，及早介入可帶來較佳的成效。

⚠️ suggest 的兩個意思
① 建議：I suggest that you leave early.（後接原形動詞）
② 顯示、指出：The data suggest that...（學術與新聞語境最常見）

閱讀測驗中，「The passage suggests...」是「文章指出／暗示」，不是「文章建議」。這是很常見的誤解。
34
Access to the server room is ____ to authorized personnel only.
(A) restricted　(B) resisted　(C) resigned　(D) resumed
詳解
答案：(A) restricted 限制
中譯：伺服器機房僅限經授權人員進入。

資安機房標示的標準英文：Access restricted to authorized personnel only.
相關：authorized 經授權的｜unauthorized access 未經授權存取｜access control 存取控制
35
The city council voted ____ of the new environmental regulations.
(A) in favor　(B) in charge　(C) in place　(D) in case
詳解
答案：(A) in favor（of）贊成
中譯：市議會投票贊成新的環保法規。

in + 名詞片語整理：
in favor of贊成
in charge of負責
in place of代替
in case of萬一、若遇
in accordance with依照（公文高頻）
in response to因應
36
The training program is designed to ____ employees' digital skills.
(A) enhance　(B) enclose　(C) encounter　(D) endure
詳解
答案：(A) enhance 提升
中譯：這項訓練計畫旨在提升員工的數位技能。

「提升」家族：enhance、improve、boost、strengthen、upgrade
enhance 語氣較正式，公文與政策文件最常用。
其他：(B) 附上｜(C) 遭遇｜(D) 忍受
37
Many elderly residents are ____ to using online services without assistance.
(A) reluctant　(B) relevant　(C) reliable　(D) related
詳解
答案：(A) reluctant 不情願的
中譯：許多年長居民在沒有協助的情況下，不願意使用線上服務。

搭配：be reluctant to V（注意：本題原句用 to using，屬 be reluctant + to + N/V-ing 的口語變體；標準用法為 be reluctant to use）
近義：hesitant、unwilling｜反義：eager、willing
38
The report highlights the ____ between policy goals and actual outcomes.
(A) discrepancy　(B) discipline　(C) discovery　(D) discount
詳解
答案：(A) discrepancy 落差、不一致
中譯：報告凸顯了政策目標與實際成效之間的落差。

解題線索：between A and B → 空格必是表示「兩者關係」的名詞（差距、關聯、對比）。
同類字：gap 落差｜correlation 相關性｜contrast 對比｜inconsistency 不一致
39
All personal data must be handled ____ with the Personal Data Protection Act.
(A) in accordance　(B) in addition　(C) in advance　(D) in general
詳解
答案：(A) in accordance（with）依照
中譯：所有個人資料均須依照《個人資料保護法》處理。

這是公文英文最高頻的片語之一：in accordance with the law / regulations / guidelines
同義：pursuant to（更正式）｜under｜in compliance with
其他：(B) 此外｜(C) 事先｜(D) 一般而言
40
The committee reached a ____ after three hours of discussion.
(A) consensus　(B) consequence　(C) constitution　(D) construction
詳解
答案：(A) consensus 共識
中譯：經過三小時的討論，委員會達成共識。

搭配：reach / build / achieve a consensus
其他：(B) consequence 後果｜(C) constitution 憲法／體質｜(D) construction 建設
(B) 是很好的誘答——「討論三小時後達成後果」語意不通，靠語感即可刪除。

B. 文法（41～70 題）

41時態
The department ____ the new system since last March.
(A) uses　(B) used　(C) has been using　(D) will use
詳解
答案：(C) has been using
中譯：該部門自去年三月起就一直使用這套新系統。

關鍵字：since → 表示「從過去某時間點持續到現在」→ 現在完成式或現在完成進行式。
「一直在使用」強調持續動作，故用完成進行式最佳。

時間副詞與時態對照（背這張表）：
since / for / so far / up to now現在完成式
yesterday / last / ago / in 2020過去式
by the time / by next year完成式
now / currently / at present現在（進行）式
42被動
The annual report ____ to the ministry last Friday.
(A) submitted　(B) was submitted　(C) has submitted　(D) submits
詳解
答案：(B) was submitted
中譯：年度報告已於上週五呈送該部。

兩個判斷：
① 語態：報告是「被」呈送的，不會自己送 → 被動
② 時態：last Friday → 過去式
→ was + p.p.

💡 判斷主動被動的一句話
問：「主詞是做這個動作的人，還是被做的東西？」
報告不會自己送 → 被動；官員送報告 → 主動。

公文與新聞英文大量使用被動語態，因為重點在「事情」而非「誰做的」。
43關係代名詞
The engineer ____ designed the system will attend the meeting.
(A) which　(B) who　(C) whom　(D) whose
詳解
答案：(B) who
中譯：設計這套系統的那位工程師將出席會議。

兩步判斷：
步驟判斷
① 先行詞是人還是物？engineer 是人 → 刪掉 (A) which
② 關代在子句中當主詞還是受詞？後面是 designed（動詞）→ 缺主詞 → 用 who
關係代名詞速記：
先行詞當主詞當受詞所有格
人whowhomwhose
物whichwhichwhose / of which
人或物that（但逗號後不可用 that）—
44關係代名詞
The new database, ____ was installed last year, has improved efficiency.
(A) that　(B) which　(C) what　(D) it
詳解
答案：(B) which
中譯：這套去年安裝的新資料庫，已提升了作業效率。

關鍵：空格前有「逗號」 → 這是非限定用法（補充說明），不可用 that。

選項為何錯
(A) that逗號後絕對不能用 that——這是最常考的一條規則
(C) whatwhat 本身不能有先行詞（what = the thing which）
(D) itit 是代名詞，不能引導子句（會造成兩個句子沒有連接詞）
限定 vs 非限定：沒逗號＝限定（缺了意思不完整）；有逗號＝非限定（拿掉也不影響主要意思）。
45假設語氣
If the system ____ properly maintained, the failure could have been avoided.
(A) is　(B) were　(C) had been　(D) has been
詳解
答案：(C) had been
中譯：假如這套系統當初有妥善維護，這次故障本來是可以避免的。

解題步驟：先看主要子句 → could have been avoided = would/could + have p.p.
→ 這是與過去事實相反 → if 子句必用 had + p.p.
→ 又因是被動（系統「被」維護）→ had been + p.p.

假設語氣三公式：
類型If 子句主要子句
與現在相反過去式（were）would + 原形
與過去相反had p.p.would have p.p.
與未來相反should / were to + 原形would + 原形
46動名詞
Employees should avoid ____ sensitive files to personal email accounts.
(A) to forward　(B) forwarding　(C) forward　(D) forwarded
詳解
答案：(B) forwarding
中譯：員工應避免將機敏檔案轉寄到個人電子信箱。

規則：avoid 後面必接動名詞（V-ing）

只接 V-ing只接 to V
avoid、enjoy、finish、mind、suggest、consider、practice、admit、deny、quit、risk、postponewant、decide、plan、hope、agree、expect、promise、refuse、offer、manage、afford
記法：「已經發生／正在做」的概念接 V-ing；「還沒發生／打算做」的概念接 to V。
（avoid 避免的是已在腦中的動作；decide 決定的是未來要做的事。）
47主動一致
The number of applications ____ increased by 20% this year.
(A) have　(B) has　(C) are　(D) were
詳解
答案：(B) has
中譯：今年申請案數量增加了 20%。

把介系詞片語畫掉：The number of applications → 主詞是 number（單數）。

⚠️ the number of vs a number of（每年必考）
片語意思動詞
the number of + 複數名詞…的數量單數（has / is）
a number of + 複數名詞許多…複數（have / are）
例：A number of applications have been rejected.（許多申請案被駁回）
48連接詞
____ the budget was limited, the project was completed on time.
(A) Despite　(B) Although　(C) However　(D) Because of
詳解
答案：(B) Although
中譯：儘管預算有限，該專案仍如期完成。

兩層判斷：
步驟判斷
① 語意「預算有限」vs「如期完成」→ 轉折 → 刪掉 (D) Because of
② 詞性空格後是完整子句（the budget was limited）→ 必須用連接詞
📐 三類轉折詞的用法（最高頻文法考點）
詞性字後面接例
連接詞although, though, even though, while子句（S + V）Although it rained, we went.
介系詞despite, in spite of名詞／動名詞Despite the rain, we went.
副詞however, nevertheless, therefore前面要分號或句號It rained; however, we went.
解題訣竅：先看空格後面是「子句」還是「名詞」，立刻刪掉一半選項。
49分詞構句
____ the risks involved, the committee decided to postpone the launch.
(A) Considered　(B) Considering　(C) Consider　(D) To consider
詳解
答案：(B) Considering
中譯：考量到其中涉及的風險，委員會決定延後上線。

判斷：分詞構句的隱藏主詞必須與主要子句主詞相同 → 主詞是 the committee（委員會）。
委員會是主動「考量」風險 → 用 V-ing。

關係用法例
主動V-ingReviewing the data, she found an error.
被動p.p.Written in 2020, the report is outdated.
⚠️ 懸垂分詞（Dangling Modifier）
Walking down the street, the building looked impressive. ✘
→ 字面上變成「大樓在走路」。分詞的主詞必須與主要子句一致，這是常考的改錯題。
50介系詞
The delay was ____ a shortage of qualified personnel.
(A) result from　(B) due to　(C) because　(D) result in
詳解
答案：(B) due to
中譯：延誤肇因於合格人力短缺。

兩個判斷：
① 空格前是 was（be 動詞）→ 需要形容詞片語或介系詞片語
② 空格後是名詞片語 a shortage → 不能用連接詞 (C) because

表達後接方向
due to / owing to / because of名詞因為（後面是原因）
because / since / as子句因為
result from名詞起因於（後面是原因）
result in / lead to名詞導致（後面是結果）
(A) 錯在文法（was result from 不通）；(D) 錯在方向（result in 後面該接結果，但這裡是原因）。
51比較
The more data an organization collects, ____ its responsibility to protect it.
(A) the great　(B) the greater　(C) greater　(D) the greatest
詳解
答案：(B) the greater
中譯：一個組織蒐集的資料越多，保護這些資料的責任就越大。

句型：The + 比較級 ..., the + 比較級 ...（越…就越…）
前半已是 The more → 後半必須是 the + 比較級 → the greater。

其他必背比較句型：
as + 原級 + as和…一樣
not as/so + 原級 + as不如…
比較級 + than any other + 單數名詞比其他任何…都…
the + 最高級 + in/of最…的
52時態
By the end of next year, the agency ____ all its systems to the cloud.
(A) migrates　(B) migrated　(C) will have migrated　(D) has migrated
詳解
答案：(C) will have migrated
中譯：到明年年底，該機關將已把所有系統遷移至雲端。

關鍵：By the end of next year → 「到未來某個時間點為止，動作將已完成」→ 未來完成式（will have + p.p.）

時間標記時態
By + 未來時間未來完成式（will have p.p.）
By + 過去時間過去完成式（had p.p.）
By now / so far現在完成式（have p.p.）
看到 by ＋ 時間點，就想到完成式。
53語態
Applicants ____ to provide proof of residence when submitting the form.
(A) require　(B) are required　(C) requiring　(D) requirement
詳解
答案：(B) are required
中譯：申請人於繳交表格時須提供居住證明。

判斷：申請人是「被要求」的一方 → 被動。
be required to V 是公文英文的高頻句型（＝須…）。

同類「被動＋to V」句型：
be required to須…
be expected to預期應…
be entitled to有權…
be obliged to有義務…
be prohibited from V-ing禁止…（注意用 from + V-ing）
54代名詞
Each of the departments ____ its own security policy.
(A) have　(B) has　(C) are having　(D) were having
詳解
答案：(B) has
中譯：各部門各有其自己的資安政策。

規則：each / every / either / neither 一律視為單數。
Each of the departments → 主詞是 Each（單數） → has。

一律單數一律複數
each、every、either、neither、one、everyone、somebody、nobodyboth、few、several、many
另注意：後面的所有格代名詞也要一致 → its（單數），不是 their。
55不定詞
The purpose of the training is ____ awareness of information security.
(A) raise　(B) to raise　(C) raising　(D) raised
詳解
答案：(B) to raise
中譯：這項訓練的目的是提升資訊安全意識。

句型：The purpose is to V（目的是要…）——不定詞表「目的」。
(C) raising 文法上也可通（動名詞當補語），但在「purpose / aim / goal is」的句型中，慣用不定詞。

相關句型：
The aim / purpose / goal is to V目的是…
in order to V / so as to V為了…
be designed to V旨在…
be intended to V意在…
56冠詞
She was appointed ____ director of the information center last month.
(A) a　(B) an　(C) the　(D) 不填
詳解
答案：(D) 不填
中譯：她於上個月獲派任為資訊中心主任。

規則：表示「獨一無二的職位」時，用於 as / become / appoint / elect 之後的頭銜不加冠詞。

例說明
He was elected president.當選總統（不加 the）
She serves as chair of the committee.擔任主席
The president gave a speech.當作一般名詞使用時要加
判準：職位當「頭銜」用不加冠詞；當「那個人」用要加。
57語態
The new guidelines ____ to all government agencies starting next month.
(A) will apply　(B) will be applied　(C) applies　(D) applying
詳解
答案：(A) will apply
中譯：新的指導原則自下個月起適用於所有政府機關。

⚠️ apply 的兩種用法（易錯）
用法意思語態
A applies to BA 適用於 B主動（規則自己「適用」）
apply for a job申請主動
A is applied to BA 被施加於 B被動（指「使用某方法」）
本題是「規則適用於機關」→ 用主動 apply to。
(B) will be applied 意思會變成「這些原則被套用上去」，語意不自然。
時態：starting next month → 未來式。
58倒裝
Not only ____ the system faster, but it also costs less to maintain.
(A) is　(B) it is　(C) is it　(D) does
詳解
答案：(C) is it
中譯：這套系統不僅速度更快，維護成本也更低。

規則：否定副詞置於句首時，主要子句必須倒裝（助動詞／be 動詞提到主詞前）。
Not only is it faster, but it also costs less.

需要倒裝的句首詞：
Not only... but also不僅…而且
Never / Seldom / Rarely / Hardly從不／很少／幾乎不
Only when / Only after / Only then只有當…才
No sooner... than一…就
記法：句首出現「否定或限定」的副詞，後面就要倒裝。
59名詞
The manager asked for more ____ before making a decision.
(A) informations　(B) information　(C) an information　(D) informations'
詳解
答案：(B) information
中譯：主管在做決定前要求提供更多資訊。

規則：information 是不可數名詞——沒有複數形、不加 a/an。

常考的不可數名詞（絕不加 s）
information、advice、equipment、furniture、luggage、news、research、evidence、knowledge、progress、software、feedback、staff（集合）
要表示「一則」時用：a piece of information｜an item of equipment｜a piece of advice

「informations」是中文母語者最常犯的錯誤之一。
60語氣助動詞
All staff ____ complete the security training by the end of June.
(A) must　(B) must to　(C) have　(D) should to
詳解
答案：(A) must
中譯：全體同仁須於六月底前完成資安訓練。

規則：助動詞後面接原形動詞，中間不加 to → 刪掉 (B)(D)。
(C) have 若要表「必須」須寫成 have to。

助動詞語氣強度用法
must最強（說話者的要求）+ 原形
have to強（外在規定）+ 原形
should / ought to建議+ 原形
may / might可能、允許+ 原形
公文英文中的 shall：法律條文用 shall 表強制義務（＝應、須），不是「將要」。
61動詞形式
The committee suggested that the deadline ____ extended by two weeks.
(A) is　(B) be　(C) will be　(D) was
詳解
答案：(B) be
中譯：委員會建議期限應延長兩週。

規則：表「建議、要求、命令、堅持」的動詞，其 that 子句用原形動詞（假設語氣現在式），should 可省略。
→ suggested that the deadline (should) be extended

這類動詞（口訣：一二三四）
suggest、recommend、propose、insist、demand、require、request、order、advise、urge
相同規則的形容詞句型：It is essential / necessary / important that S + (should) + 原形

這一條是國考文法的常勝軍，且很多人不知道，答對率偏低——記起來就是分數。
62副詞
The team responded ____ to the security incident.
(A) quick　(B) quickly　(C) quicker　(D) quickness
詳解
答案：(B) quickly
中譯：團隊迅速地回應了這起資安事件。

判斷：修飾動詞（responded）→ 用副詞。

修飾對象用例
名詞形容詞a quick response
動詞、形容詞、副詞副詞responded quickly
be 動詞後（主詞補語）形容詞The response was quick.
易錯：感官動詞（look, sound, feel, taste, smell）後面接形容詞——It looks good（不是 well）。
63關係副詞
This is the office ____ the records are stored.
(A) which　(B) that　(C) where　(D) what
詳解
答案：(C) where
中譯：這就是存放這些紀錄的辦公室。

判斷方法：看關代後面的子句「完不完整」
後面子句用本題
不完整（缺主詞或受詞）關係代名詞（which / that / who）—
完整（主詞受詞都有）關係副詞（where / when / why / how）✔ the records are stored 是完整句
關係副詞對照：地點 → where｜時間 → when｜理由 → why｜方法 → how

若要改用 which，必須補上介系詞：the office in which the records are stored。
64平行結構
The system is designed to be secure, reliable, and ____.
(A) easy to use　(B) using easily　(C) it is easy　(D) with ease
詳解
答案：(A) easy to use
中譯：這套系統的設計目標是安全、可靠且易於使用。

規則：平行結構——用 and / or 連接的成分，詞性與形式必須一致。
前兩項是形容詞（secure, reliable）→ 第三項也必須是形容詞片語 → easy to use。

✔ 正確✘ 錯誤
reading, writing, and speakingreading, writing, and to speak
to plan, to design, and to implementto plan, designing, and implementation
解題技巧：看到 and / or，就往前找它連接的是什麼形式，照著填。
65時態
When the technician arrived, the server ____ down for two hours.
(A) is　(B) was　(C) had been　(D) will be
詳解
答案：(C) had been
中譯：技術人員抵達時，伺服器已經停機兩小時了。

規則：過去的兩個動作，先發生的用過去完成式。
時間軸：
伺服器停機 ────2 小時────► 技術人員抵達 ────► 現在
had been down　　　　　　 arrived
（過去完成式）　　　　　　　　　（過去式）
另一個線索：for two hours（持續一段時間）→ 完成式。

口訣：過去的過去，用過去完成式。
66介系詞
The department is responsible ____ maintaining the network infrastructure.
(A) of　(B) for　(C) to　(D) with
詳解
答案：(B) for
中譯：該部門負責維護網路基礎建設。

固定搭配：be responsible for + N / V-ing

形容詞 + 介系詞（必背）意思
be responsible for負責
be aware of知悉
be familiar with熟悉
be capable of有能力
be eligible for符合資格
be subject to受…拘束、須經…（公文高頻）
be consistent with與…一致
be exempt from免除
67句構
It is essential that every employee ____ the confidentiality agreement.
(A) signs　(B) sign　(C) signed　(D) will sign
詳解
答案：(B) sign
中譯：每一位員工都必須簽署保密協議，這是必要的。

規則：It is essential / necessary / important / imperative / vital that S + (should) + 原形動詞
→ should 省略，故用原形 sign（即使主詞 every employee 是單數，也不加 s）。

這與第 61 題（suggest that...）是同一條規則，只是一個用動詞、一個用形容詞觸發。

💡 兩個觸發詞群一起記
動詞：suggest, recommend, propose, insist, demand, require, request, order
形容詞：essential, necessary, important, imperative, vital, crucial
→ 後面的 that 子句一律用原形動詞。
68語態
A total of 1,200 applications ____ so far this year.
(A) has received　(B) have received　(C) have been received　(D) has been receiving
詳解
答案：(C) have been received
中譯：今年至今已收到總計 1,200 件申請。

三個判斷：
面向判斷
語態申請案是「被」收到的 → 被動 → 刪 (A)(B)
時態so far → 現在完成式
單複數A total of + 複數名詞 → 視為複數 → 用 have
⚠️ A total of 的單複數
A total of 1,200 applications have been received.（看後面的名詞）
這與 The number of（一律單數）規則相反，很容易混淆。
69連接詞
The system crashed; ____, all data was successfully recovered from the backup.
(A) therefore　(B) however　(C) moreover　(D) for example
詳解
答案：(B) however
中譯：系統當機了；然而，所有資料都已從備份順利還原。

邏輯判斷：系統當機（壞消息） vs 資料成功還原（好消息） → 轉折。

邏輯關係常用詞判斷方式
轉折however, nevertheless, in contrast, on the contrary前後方向相反
因果therefore, thus, consequently, as a result後句是前句的結果
遞進moreover, furthermore, in addition, besides同方向再加一項
舉例for example, for instance, such as後句是前句的例子
克漏字最常考這一類。做法：先讀前後兩句，判斷「同向」還是「反向」，立刻刪一半。
70綜合
Neither the manager nor the engineers ____ aware of the vulnerability.
(A) is　(B) was　(C) were　(D) has been
詳解
答案：(C) were
中譯：無論是主管還是工程師們，都不知道這個漏洞的存在。

規則：either...or / neither...nor / not only...but also → 動詞與最靠近的主詞一致（就近原則）。
最靠近動詞的是 the engineers（複數）→ 用 were。

句型動詞依據例
either A or B就近原則（看 B）Either you or he is wrong.
neither A nor BNeither he nor they are here.
not only A but also BNot only I but also they were late.
A as well as B看 A（前者）He, as well as they, is going.
A and B複數He and she are here.
特別注意 as well as 是唯一「看前面」的——常考陷阱。

C. 克漏字（4 篇，71～90 題）

克漏字 1數位政府 · 71～75
Governments around the world are turning to digital technology to improve public services. In Taiwan, many applications that once required a personal visit to a government office can now be completed online (71) a few minutes. This shift has reduced waiting times and lowered administrative costs.

However, digitalization is not simply a matter of moving paper forms onto websites. If citizens still have to enter the same information three times on three different pages, the inconvenience has merely been (72) from the counter to the screen. Genuine digital transformation requires agencies to share data with one another so that citizens need only "tell us once."

(73), digital services can create new barriers. Elderly residents and those living in remote areas may lack the devices, the connection, or the confidence to use them. For this reason, most experts (74) that traditional counter services be maintained alongside online options.

Ultimately, technology is only a tool. What determines the quality of public service is (75) the officials behind the system remember that a real person is waiting on the other end.
(71)(A) among　(B) within　(C) between　(D) along
(72)(A) removed　(B) improved　(C) relocated　(D) reduced
(73)(A) Therefore　(B) For example　(C) Nevertheless　(D) In addition
(74)(A) recommend　(B) recommends　(C) recommending　(D) to recommend
(75)(A) what　(B) whether　(C) which　(D) that
看詳解與全文中譯
📋 答案：71.(B)　72.(C)　73.(C)　74.(A)　75.(B)
題答案解析
71(B) within時間空格：「在幾分鐘之內」用 within + 時間。among 用於三者以上之間；between 用於兩者之間；along 沿著
72(C) relocated語意空格：句意是「不便只是從櫃檯搬到了螢幕」——後面有 from the counter to the screen（從…到…）→ 必是移動的動詞。(A) removed 是「移除」不是「移動」；(B)(D) 與「merely（只是）」的語氣矛盾
73(C) Nevertheless連接詞空格：前段講數位化的正面價值，本段講它製造新障礙 → 轉折。(A) 因果、(B) 舉例、(D) 遞進 皆不合
74(A) recommend文法空格：主詞 most experts 是複數 → 動詞不加 s。（注意：後面的 that 子句用 be maintained 原形，正是「recommend that + 原形」的規則）
75(B) whether句構空格：What determines... is ____ the officials remember...——後面是完整子句且語意為「是否記得」→ 用 whether。what 後面須接不完整子句；which 需有選項範圍；that 引導的名詞子句語意較弱且此處不通
📖 全文中譯 　　世界各國政府正轉向以數位科技提升公共服務。在臺灣，許多過去必須親自前往機關辦理的申請案，如今只需幾分鐘之內就能在線上完成。這樣的轉變縮短了等候時間，也降低了行政成本。

　　然而，數位化並不只是把紙本表單搬到網站上而已。如果民眾仍必須在三個不同的頁面上填寫三次相同的資料，那麼這份不便只不過是從櫃檯被搬到了螢幕。真正的數位轉型，需要各機關彼此交換資料，讓民眾「只需說一次」。

　　然而，數位服務也可能製造新的障礙。年長的居民與居住在偏遠地區的人，可能缺少設備、缺少網路連線，或缺少使用的信心。正因如此，多數專家建議，傳統的臨櫃服務應與線上選項並行保留。

　　歸根結柢，科技只是一項工具。真正決定公共服務品質的，是系統背後的公務人員是否記得：螢幕另一端等待的，是一個真實的人。
📝 值得抄進單字本的字 turn to 轉向｜shift 轉變｜merely 僅僅｜genuine 真正的｜barrier 障礙｜remote areas 偏遠地區｜alongside 與…並行｜ultimately 最終
克漏字 2資訊安全 · 76～80
Ransomware attacks have become one of the most serious threats facing public agencies today. In a typical case, an employee opens an attachment in a (76) email, and malicious software quietly installs itself on the computer. Over the following days or weeks, the attacker moves through the network, collecting passwords and identifying valuable data.

When the attack is finally launched, files across dozens of servers are encrypted within hours. The agency is then asked to pay a ransom (77) the decryption key.

Security experts agree that the single most important defense is a reliable backup. (78), many organizations discover too late that their backups were connected to the same network and were encrypted along with everything else. For this reason, at least one copy should be kept offline or in an (79) form that cannot be altered.

Prevention also matters. Keeping software updated, limiting administrative privileges, and requiring multi-factor authentication can all make an attack far more (80) to carry out.
(76)(A) phishing　(B) fishing　(C) finishing　(D) flashing
(77)(A) in return for　(B) in spite of　(C) in place of　(D) in charge of
(78)(A) Similarly　(B) Unfortunately　(C) Consequently　(D) Additionally
(79)(A) immediate　(B) immune　(C) immutable　(D) imminent
(80)(A) easier　(B) difficult　(C) likely　(D) common
看詳解與全文中譯
📋 答案：76.(A)　77.(A)　78.(B)　79.(C)　80.(B)
題答案解析
76(A) phishing資安專有名詞：phishing email 釣魚郵件。(B) fishing 是釣魚（真的釣魚）；(C) 完成；(D) 閃爍——拼字只差一個字母，是典型的資安字彙題
77(A) in return for片語空格：「支付贖金以換取解密金鑰」→ in return for（作為…的交換）。(B) 儘管；(C) 代替；(D) 負責
78(B) Unfortunately連接詞空格：前句說「備份是最重要的防禦」，後句說「很多組織太晚才發現備份也被加密了」→ 這是不幸的轉折。(A) 類似地、(C) 因此、(D) 此外，方向都不對
79(C) immutable語意＋專業字：後面有 that cannot be altered（無法被更動）→ 這就是空格的定義 → immutable 不可變的（正是資安術語「不可變備份」）。(A) 立即的；(B) 免疫的；(D) 迫在眉睫的
80(B) difficult語意方向：本段講的都是防護措施（更新、限縮權限、MFA）→ 目的是讓攻擊更難執行。(A) easier 方向相反；(C)(D) 語意不通
📖 全文中譯 　　勒索軟體攻擊已成為當今公務機關所面臨最嚴重的威脅之一。在典型的案例中，某位員工開啟了一封釣魚郵件中的附件，惡意軟體便悄悄地在該電腦上完成安裝。在接下來的數日或數週間，攻擊者於網路內部移動，蒐集密碼並鎖定有價值的資料。

　　當攻擊最終發動時，數十台伺服器上的檔案會在數小時內遭到加密。機關接著會被要求支付贖金，以換取解密金鑰。

　　資安專家一致認為，最重要的單一防線就是可靠的備份。不幸的是，許多組織往往為時已晚才發現：他們的備份也連在同一個網路上，因而與其他所有資料一併遭到加密。正因如此，至少應保留一份離線副本，或以無法被更動的不可變形式保存。

　　預防同樣重要。保持軟體更新、限縮管理者權限，以及要求多因子驗證，都能使攻擊的執行變得困難得多。
📝 資安必背字（本篇密度極高） ransomware 勒索軟體｜malicious software 惡意軟體｜attachment 附件｜encrypt 加密｜decryption key 解密金鑰｜ransom 贖金｜backup 備份｜offline 離線｜immutable 不可變的｜administrative privileges 管理者權限｜multi-factor authentication 多因子驗證
💡 這篇對你特別友善 你在專業科目已經讀過這整套內容（勒索軟體、離線備份、MFA、最小權限）。
即使英文單字不全認識，靠背景知識也能推出大部分答案——這就是資訊處理考生在英文科的隱藏優勢。
克漏字 3高齡社會 · 81～85
Taiwan officially became a "super-aged society" as the proportion of residents aged 65 and over passed twenty percent. This demographic change presents challenges that reach far (81) healthcare.

Local governments must rethink how services are designed. Public buildings need ramps and elevators; websites need larger text and simpler navigation; and staff need training in how to communicate (82) with older visitors.

At the same time, an aging population is not only a burden. Many people in their sixties and seventies remain healthy, experienced, and willing to work. Some cities have therefore created programs that (83) older residents to serve as community volunteers or part-time mentors.

The key, according to policy researchers, is to stop treating age as a single category. A 65-year-old and a 90-year-old have very different needs, and policies that (84) this distinction are unlikely to succeed.

Preparing for a super-aged society is (85) a matter of building more nursing homes. It requires rethinking transportation, housing, employment, and the design of every public service.
(81)(A) beyond　(B) below　(C) besides　(D) between
(82)(A) patient　(B) patience　(C) patiently　(D) patients
(83)(A) prevent　(B) encourage　(C) require　(D) forbid
(84)(A) ignore　(B) respect　(C) create　(D) explain
(85)(A) only　(B) not merely　(C) always　(D) never
看詳解與全文中譯
📋 答案：81.(A)　82.(C)　83.(B)　84.(A)　85.(B)
題答案解析
81(A) beyondreach far beyond healthcare＝「影響遠遠超出醫療照護的範圍」。固定搭配 far beyond；besides 是「除…之外還有」，語氣較弱且不與 far 連用
82(C) patiently詞性題：修飾動詞 communicate → 用副詞。(A) 形容詞／(B) 名詞（耐心）／(D) 名詞複數（病人）——patient 一字兩義，是很好的誘答設計
83(B) encourage語意方向：本段講的是「高齡人口不只是負擔」，且後面說他們擔任志工或導師 → 政策應是鼓勵。(A) 阻止、(D) 禁止方向相反；(C) require（要求）語氣過強且不合志願服務的性質
84(A) ignore邏輯題：前句說 65 歲與 90 歲需求截然不同 → 「忽視這項差異的政策不太可能成功」。若填 respect（尊重），則整句語意矛盾——尊重差異的政策應該會成功
85(B) not merely語氣題：下一句說「還需要重新思考交通、住宅、就業…」→ 表示不只是蓋安養院 → not merely（不僅僅是）。(A) only 語意相反；(C)(D) 不通
📖 全文中譯 　　隨著 65 歲以上居民占比超過兩成，臺灣正式邁入「超高齡社會」。這項人口結構的變化所帶來的挑戰，遠遠超出醫療照護的範圍。

　　地方政府必須重新思考服務的設計方式。公共建築需要坡道與電梯；網站需要更大的字體與更簡潔的操作動線；而工作人員則需要接受訓練，學習如何耐心地與年長民眾溝通。

　　同時，高齡人口並不只是一種負擔。許多六、七十歲的人依然健康、經驗豐富且有意願工作。因此，有些城市已推出方案，鼓勵年長居民擔任社區志工或兼職導師。

　　政策研究者認為，關鍵在於不再把「年齡」當成單一類別來看待。65 歲與 90 歲的人需求截然不同，而忽視這項差異的政策，不太可能成功。

　　為超高齡社會做準備，不僅僅是興建更多安養機構而已。它需要我們重新思考交通、住宅、就業，以及每一項公共服務的設計方式。
📝 值得抄進單字本的字 super-aged society 超高齡社會｜proportion 比例｜demographic 人口結構的｜ramp 坡道｜navigation 操作動線｜burden 負擔｜mentor 導師｜distinction 區別｜nursing home 安養機構
克漏字 4人工智慧 · 86～90
Generative artificial intelligence has spread rapidly through workplaces, and government offices are no exception. Officials now use these tools to draft documents, summarize reports, and translate materials. The gains in efficiency are real, but so (86) the risks.

The most immediate danger is not dramatic. It is simply that an employee, hoping to save time, pastes confidential material into a public AI service. Once submitted, that information may be stored by the provider or even used to train future models. (87), agencies should clearly prohibit the entry of personal data and classified documents into public tools.

A second problem is that these systems can produce answers that sound confident but are factually wrong — a phenomenon often called "hallucination." A citation may look perfectly formatted and still refer to a regulation that does not exist. Every output therefore needs to be (88) by a human before use.

Some organizations have responded by banning such tools altogether. Researchers warn, however, that outright bans often (89) employees to use them secretly on personal devices, which makes the problem harder to manage.

A better approach is to provide approved tools, set clear boundaries, and remind staff that responsibility for the final product always (90) with the person who signs it.
(86)(A) are　(B) is　(C) do　(D) have
(87)(A) Nevertheless　(B) For this reason　(C) By contrast　(D) For example
(88)(A) generated　(B) verified　(C) translated　(D) submitted
(89)(A) prevent　(B) allow　(C) drive　(D) remind
(90)(A) rests　(B) rises　(C) results　(D) resists
看詳解與全文中譯
📋 答案：86.(A)　87.(B)　88.(B)　89.(C)　90.(A)
題答案解析
86(A) are倒裝＋一致：so + be 動詞 + 主詞（…也是如此）。主詞是 the risks（複數）→ 用 are。句意：效率的提升是真的，但風險也是真的
87(B) For this reason連接詞空格：前句說「資料可能被業者留存或用於訓練」（原因），後句說「機關應明文禁止」（結果）→ 因果
88(B) verified語意：前面講 AI 會產生「聽起來很有把握卻是錯的」答案 → 產出必須經人工查證。(A) 產生（已經發生了）、(C) 翻譯、(D) 提交，皆不解決正確性問題
89(C) drive語意：drive someone to V＝迫使／驅使某人去做。句意：全面禁令反而迫使員工偷偷在私人裝置上使用。(A) prevent 意思相反；(B) allow 語意不通（禁令不會「允許」）；(D) remind 不合搭配
90(A) rests片語：responsibility rests with + 人＝責任歸屬於某人。這是公文與法律英文的固定說法。(B) 上升｜(C) 導致（要配 in/from）｜(D) 抵抗
📖 全文中譯 　　生成式人工智慧已迅速滲透各行各業的工作場所，政府機關也不例外。公務人員如今會使用這些工具來草擬文件、摘要報告與翻譯資料。效率上的提升是真實的，但風險也同樣真實。

　　最迫切的危險並不戲劇化。它不過是：某位員工為了節省時間，把機敏資料貼進了公開的 AI 服務。一旦送出，那些資訊就可能被業者留存，甚至被用於訓練未來的模型。正因如此，機關應明確禁止將個人資料與機密文件輸入公開工具。

　　第二個問題是，這些系統可能產生「聽起來很有把握、事實上卻是錯的」答案——這種現象常被稱為「幻覺」。一則引註可能格式完美無瑕，所指向的卻是一條根本不存在的法規。因此，每一項產出在使用前都必須經過人工查證。

　　有些組織的因應方式是全面禁用這類工具。然而研究者警告，一刀切的禁令往往反而迫使員工在私人裝置上偷偷使用，這反倒讓問題更難管理。

　　較好的做法是：提供經核可的工具、劃定清楚的界線，並提醒同仁——最終成果的責任，永遠歸屬於那位署名的人。
📝 值得抄進單字本的字 generative AI 生成式 AI｜draft 草擬｜summarize 摘要｜confidential 機密的｜classified documents 機密文件｜hallucination 幻覺｜citation 引註｜outright ban 全面禁令｜boundaries 界線｜rest with 歸屬於
💡 一魚兩吃 這篇的內容與你在資通網路與安全申論 13（生成式 AI 資安）幾乎完全一致。
讀英文的同時也在複習專業科目——這是資訊處理考生準備英文最有效率的方式：專挑科技與資安題材練閱讀。

D. 閱讀測驗（8 篇，91～124 題）

閱讀 1開放資料 · 91～94
　　Open data refers to information that governments publish in formats anyone can freely use, reuse, and redistribute. Over the past decade, many countries have released datasets covering transportation schedules, air quality readings, budget records, and public facility locations.

　　The benefits are considerable. Developers have built applications that tell commuters when the next bus will arrive; researchers have used environmental data to identify pollution patterns; and journalists have examined spending records to hold agencies accountable. None of these outcomes required the government itself to build anything beyond the dataset.

　　Yet publishing data is not as simple as uploading a file. A dataset released only as a scanned PDF cannot be processed by software, and one that is never updated quickly becomes misleading. Agencies must also consider re-identification risk: even after names are removed, a combination of birth date, gender, and postal code may be enough to identify an individual. When a village reports only one or two low-income households, publishing that figure may effectively expose who they are.

　　For this reason, experienced practitioners argue that open data programs need three things before publication: a machine-readable format, a clear update schedule, and a formal review of privacy risk. Without them, an open data policy may generate impressive statistics about the number of datasets released, while producing very little public value.
91. What is the main idea of this passage?
(A) Open data has failed in most countries.
(B) Open data brings real benefits but requires careful implementation.
(C) Governments should stop publishing personal data.
(D) Only developers benefit from open data.
92. According to the passage, why is a scanned PDF problematic?
(A) It is too large to download.　(B) It cannot be processed by software.
(C) It contains personal information.　(D) It is updated too frequently.
93. The example of "one or two low-income households" is used to illustrate
(A) the cost of collecting data　(B) the difficulty of updating datasets
(C) the risk of re-identification　(D) the benefits of transparency
94. What can be inferred from the last paragraph?
(A) Counting released datasets is a poor measure of success.
(B) Open data policies should be abandoned.
(C) Privacy review is unnecessary for most datasets.
(D) Machine-readable formats are rarely available.
看詳解與全文中譯
📋 答案：91.(B)　92.(B)　93.(C)　94.(A)
題答案解析
91
主旨題		(B)文章結構是第 2 段講好處、第 3～4 段講必要條件 → 「有價值，但需要謹慎執行」正好涵蓋全文。
其他錯在哪：(A)「大多數國家失敗」文章沒說；(C) 太窄（只是第 3 段的一部分）且說反了；(D) 太窄（第 2 段還提到研究者與記者）
92
細節題		(B)用關鍵詞 scanned PDF 定位 → 原文：cannot be processed by software。幾乎原句照抄，是送分題
93
目的題		(C)該例子出現在 re-identification risk 這句之後，功能是舉例說明它。
技巧：問「這個例子是用來說明什麼」時，答案幾乎都在例子的前一句
94
推論題		(A)末段：「若缺少這三樣，開放資料政策可能產出漂亮的『釋出資料集數量』統計，卻幾乎沒有公共價值」→ 可推知「用數量衡量成效並不恰當」。
其他錯在哪：(B) 文章主張改善而非放棄；(C) 說反了；(D) 文章沒討論可得性
📖 全文中譯 　　開放資料指的是：政府以任何人都能自由使用、再利用與再散布的格式所公布的資訊。過去十年間，許多國家已釋出涵蓋交通時刻、空氣品質監測值、預算紀錄與公共設施位置的資料集。

　　其效益相當可觀。開發者打造出能告知通勤者下一班公車何時抵達的應用程式；研究人員運用環境資料辨識出汙染的模式；記者則透過檢視支出紀錄來監督機關的課責。這些成果沒有任何一項需要政府自己在資料集之外再多建置什麼。

　　然而，公布資料並不像上傳一個檔案那麼簡單。僅以掃描 PDF 形式釋出的資料集，無法被軟體處理；而從不更新的資料集，很快就會變得具有誤導性。機關還必須考量重新識別風險：即使姓名已被移除，出生日期、性別與郵遞區號的組合，仍可能足以指認出某個人。當某個村里僅通報一至兩戶低收入戶時，公布這個數字實際上等於揭露了他們是誰。

　　正因如此，經驗豐富的實務工作者主張，開放資料計畫在公布前需要三樣東西：機器可讀的格式、明確的更新排程，以及正式的隱私風險審查。少了這三樣，開放資料政策或許能產出關於「釋出了多少資料集」的漂亮統計數字，實際創造的公共價值卻少得可憐。
📝 值得抄進單字本的字 redistribute 再散布｜dataset 資料集｜considerable 可觀的｜commuter 通勤者｜hold sb. accountable 課責｜misleading 具誤導性的｜re-identification 重新識別｜machine-readable 機器可讀的｜practitioner 實務工作者
💡 這篇的內容你已經讀過 「小樣本揭露」與「間接識別」正是網路資安申論 11（個資保護與開放資料）的核心考點。
同一套知識，中文能寫申論、英文能做閱讀——這就是共同科目與專業科目的交集紅利。
閱讀 2遠距辦公 · 95～98
　　When large numbers of employees began working from home, many organizations discovered that their security architecture had been built on a simple assumption: everything inside the office network could be trusted, and everything outside could not. Once staff started connecting from home routers and personal laptops, that assumption collapsed.

　　The traditional response was the virtual private network, or VPN. A VPN encrypts the connection between a remote worker and the office, which protects data in transit. The difficulty is what happens next. In most traditional designs, once a user is connected, they are treated as if they were sitting at a desk inside the building — with broad access to internal systems. A single stolen password can therefore open a very large door.

　　This is why many security teams have moved toward a model known as zero trust. The principle is summarized in three words: never trust, always verify. Instead of granting access to an entire network, the system grants access to one specific application at a time, and only after checking who the user is, what device they are using, and whether that device meets security requirements.

　　Implementing zero trust is not a single purchase. It is a multi-year process that usually begins with the least glamorous step: enabling multi-factor authentication on every remote account. Security researchers consistently report that this one measure blocks the overwhelming majority of attacks that rely on stolen credentials — which is why it remains the first recommendation for any organization, regardless of budget.
95. What assumption does the passage say collapsed?
(A) That employees prefer working from home.
(B) That everything inside the office network could be trusted.
(C) That VPNs are too expensive.
(D) That passwords are always secure.
96. According to the passage, what is the main weakness of a traditional VPN?
(A) It does not encrypt data.　(B) It is difficult to install.
(C) A connected user often gains broad internal access.　(D) It requires special hardware.
97. The phrase "the least glamorous step" in the last paragraph refers to
(A) purchasing new firewalls　(B) enabling multi-factor authentication
(C) replacing all laptops　(D) hiring more security staff
98. What is the author's attitude toward multi-factor authentication?
(A) Skeptical　(B) Neutral　(C) Supportive　(D) Critical

(A) 明顯錯——文章第 2 段第 2 句就說 VPN 會加密
97
指代／字義題		(B)該片語後面緊接冒號：the least glamorous step: enabling multi-factor authentication。
技巧：冒號、破折號、逗號後的補充，往往就是答案
98

但注意不是「狂熱」——文章語氣仍是務實的建議，故選 supportive 而非更強烈的詞
📖 全文中譯 　　當大量員工開始在家工作時，許多組織才發現：他們的資安架構建立在一個簡單的假設之上——辦公室網路裡的一切都可以信任，外面的一切則否。一旦同仁開始從家用路由器與個人筆電連線，這個假設便瓦解了。

　　傳統的因應方式是虛擬私人網路，也就是 VPN。VPN 會加密遠端工作者與辦公室之間的連線，藉此保護傳輸中的資料。困難之處在於「連上之後會發生什麼」。在多數傳統設計中，使用者一旦連上，就會被當成坐在辦公大樓裡的一張辦公桌前那樣對待——擁有對內部系統的廣泛存取權。因此，一組被竊的密碼就足以打開一扇非常大的門。

　　這正是許多資安團隊轉向「零信任」模式的原因。其原則可以用三個字概括：永不信任，一律驗證。系統不再授予對整個網路的存取權，而是一次只開放一個特定的應用程式，且必須先確認使用者是誰、他使用的是什麼裝置，以及該裝置是否符合資安要求。

　　導入零信任並不是一次採購就能完成的事。它是一段通常長達數年的歷程，而起點往往是最不起眼的那一步：為每一個遠端帳號啟用多因子驗證。資安研究者一致指出，光是這一項措施，就能阻擋絕大多數倚賴竊得憑證的攻擊——這也是為什麼無論預算多寡，它始終是對任何組織的第一項建議。
📝 值得抄進單字本的字 architecture 架構｜assumption 假設｜collapse 瓦解｜in transit 傳輸中｜grant access 授予存取權｜zero trust 零信任｜glamorous 光鮮的｜overwhelming majority 絕大多數｜credentials 憑證｜regardless of 無論
閱讀 3城市韌性 · 99～102
　　Every year, cities in Taiwan face typhoons, heavy rainfall, and occasional earthquakes. Traditionally, disaster planning focused on resistance: building higher seawalls, deeper drainage, and stronger structures. In recent years, however, planners have added a second concept — resilience.

　　The distinction matters. Resistance asks how much force a system can withstand before it fails. Resilience asks a different question: when failure does occur, how quickly can the city recover, and how much damage spreads to other systems in the meantime?

　　A resilient city, for instance, does not assume that its power grid will never fail. Instead, it ensures that hospitals have generators, that water pumps have independent power sources, and that emergency communication does not depend on a single network. It also prepares people, not just infrastructure: residents who know where shelters are and how to receive official alerts recover faster than those who do not.

　　Information systems play a growing role in this shift. Sensors can warn of rising water levels; mobile alerts can reach millions within seconds; and open geographic data allows volunteers to organize relief efforts. Yet planners caution that digital tools introduce dependencies of their own. If an evacuation system relies entirely on smartphones, those without them — often the elderly, who are also the most vulnerable — may be left behind. The most resilient designs therefore keep at least one non-digital channel available, from village broadcast systems to door-to-door notification.
99. According to the passage, what does "resilience" primarily concern?
(A) Preventing all failures　(B) Recovering quickly after failure occurs
(C) Building higher seawalls　(D) Reducing construction costs
100. Why does the passage mention hospitals having generators?
(A) To show that hospitals waste electricity
(B) To illustrate preparing for the failure of another system
(C) To argue that generators are expensive
(D) To explain how power grids work
101. What concern does the passage raise about digital tools?
(A) They are too slow.　(B) They cost too much.
(C) They create new dependencies that may exclude some people.
(D) They cannot deliver alerts.
102. What would the author most likely recommend?
(A) Replacing all traditional systems with digital ones
(B) Abandoning digital alert systems
(C) Maintaining both digital and non-digital channels
(D) Focusing only on infrastructure, not people
看詳解與全文中譯
📋 答案：99.(B)　100.(B)　101.(C)　102.(C)
題答案解析

(A)(C) 是 resistance（抵抗）的定義——這題考的正是兩個概念的區別
100
目的題		(B)該例出現在 does not assume that its power grid will never fail 之後 → 功能是說明「為另一個系統的失效預做準備」
101
細節題		(C)末段：digital tools introduce dependencies of their own，並舉「沒有智慧型手機的長者可能被遺漏」為例

📖 全文中譯 　　臺灣的城市每年都要面對颱風、豪雨，以及偶發的地震。傳統上，防災規劃著重於「抵抗力」：興建更高的海堤、更深的排水系統，以及更堅固的結構。然而近年來，規劃者又加入了第二個概念——韌性。

　　這個區分很重要。抵抗力問的是：一個系統在失效之前能承受多大的力量。韌性問的則是另一個問題：當失效確實發生時，城市能多快恢復？在此期間又有多少損害會擴散到其他系統？

　　舉例來說，一座具韌性的城市不會假設它的電網永遠不會故障。相反地，它會確保醫院配有發電機、抽水站擁有獨立電源，以及緊急通訊不依賴單一網路。它同時也為「人」做準備，而不只是為基礎建設：知道避難所在哪裡、知道如何接收官方警訊的居民，恢復得比不知道的人更快。

　　資訊系統在這項轉變中扮演日益吃重的角色。感測器可預警水位上升；行動警訊可在數秒內觸及數百萬人；開放的地理資料則讓志工得以組織救援行動。然而規劃者提醒，數位工具本身也帶來了新的依賴。如果一套疏散系統完全倚賴智慧型手機，那些沒有手機的人——往往是長者，也正是最脆弱的一群——就可能被遺落在後。因此，最具韌性的設計，會至少保留一條非數位的管道，從村里廣播系統到挨家挨戶的通知都算在內。
📝 值得抄進單字本的字 resistance 抵抗力｜resilience 韌性｜withstand 承受｜power grid 電網｜generator 發電機｜shelter 避難所｜evacuation 疏散｜dependency 依賴｜vulnerable 脆弱的｜relief efforts 救援行動
💡 一魚兩吃 「韌性 vs 抵抗力」的思路，和你在專業科目讀的 BCP／DRP、RTO／RPO 是同一套邏輯——不是假設不會壞，而是規劃壞掉之後多快能救回來。
閱讀 4公共採購 · 103～106
　　When a government agency needs a new information system, it rarely builds one itself. Instead, it writes a set of requirements, invites companies to bid, and awards a contract. This process is designed to ensure fairness and value for money, but it also creates a well-known problem: the agency must describe what it wants before it fully understands what is possible.

　　The consequences appear years later. A system built to a rigid specification may work exactly as written and still fail to serve users, because the specification itself was based on assumptions that turned out to be wrong. Meanwhile, because only the original vendor understands the code, the agency finds that maintenance costs rise every year and that switching suppliers would mean starting over. This condition is commonly called vendor lock-in.

　　Several remedies have been proposed. Contracts can require delivery of source code and documentation, so that another company could take over if necessary. Standard data formats can be mandated, making it easier to move information out of one system and into another. Some agencies now break large projects into smaller phases, allowing requirements to be adjusted as understanding improves.

　　None of these measures is free. Shorter phases require more procurement work, and demanding source code may raise the bid price. But experienced project managers argue that these costs are modest compared with the alternative — paying indefinitely for a system no one else can maintain.
103. What problem does the first paragraph identify?
(A) Companies refuse to bid on government contracts.
(B) Agencies must specify requirements before fully understanding what is possible.
(C) Governments build all their systems in house.
(D) Contracts are awarded without competition.
104. What is "vendor lock-in" as described in the passage?
(A) A security measure preventing unauthorized access
(B) A situation where only the original supplier can maintain the system
(C) A method of reducing maintenance costs
(D) A requirement to use open source software
105. Which of the following is NOT mentioned as a remedy?
(A) Requiring delivery of source code　(B) Mandating standard data formats
(C) Breaking projects into smaller phases　(D) Prohibiting foreign companies from bidding
106. What does the last paragraph suggest about the proposed remedies?
(A) They are free of cost.　(B) They are worth their cost.
(C) They have never been tried.　(D) They should be avoided.
看詳解與全文中譯

105
否定細節題		(D)第 3 段列出三項：source code、standard data formats、smaller phases → 對應 (A)(B)(C)。
(D) 禁止外國廠商投標，文章完全沒提——「文章沒提到」是最常見的錯誤選項類型

📖 全文中譯 　　當政府機關需要一套新的資訊系統時，它很少自己建置，而是寫出一份需求規格、邀請廠商投標，並決標簽約。這套流程的設計目的是確保公平與物有所值，但它同時也製造出一個眾所周知的問題：機關必須在尚未充分理解「什麼是做得到的」之前，就先描述出「自己想要什麼」。

　　後果會在數年後浮現。一套依照僵固規格打造的系統，可能完全照著白紙黑字運作，卻仍然無法真正服務使用者——因為那份規格本身，就建立在後來被證明是錯誤的假設之上。與此同時，由於只有原承包廠商看得懂程式碼，機關會發現維護費用逐年上漲，而更換供應商則意味著一切重來。這種情況通常被稱為「廠商鎖定」。

　　目前已有幾種因應對策。契約可以要求交付原始碼與文件，使另一家廠商必要時能夠接手。也可以強制採用標準資料格式，讓資訊更容易從一套系統移轉到另一套。有些機關現在則把大型專案拆成若干較小的階段，讓需求得以隨著理解的深化而調整。

　　這些措施沒有一項是免費的。較短的階段意味著更多的採購作業，而要求交付原始碼則可能推高投標價格。但經驗豐富的專案經理主張：相較於另一種下場——為一套沒有別人能維護的系統無限期地付費下去——這些成本其實相當有限。
📝 值得抄進單字本的字 bid 投標｜award a contract 決標｜value for money 物有所值｜specification 規格｜rigid 僵固的｜vendor 廠商｜vendor lock-in 廠商鎖定｜remedy 對策｜mandate 強制規定｜procurement 採購｜indefinitely 無限期地
💡 一魚兩吃 這篇整篇就是資訊管理的「委外管理」考點：原始碼交付、智財歸屬、資料可攜、退場條款、避免廠商鎖定。

閱讀 5循環經濟 · 107～110
　　For most of the industrial era, the economy has followed a straight line: extract raw materials, manufacture products, use them, and throw them away. This "linear" model assumes that resources are unlimited and that waste can always be placed somewhere else. Neither assumption holds today.

　　The circular economy proposes a different shape. Products are designed from the beginning to be repaired, reused, refurbished, and finally recycled, so that materials remain in circulation rather than becoming waste. The idea is not new — repair shops and second-hand markets have existed for centuries — but treating it as a design principle rather than an afterthought is a recent development.

　　Electronic waste offers a clear example. A modern smartphone contains gold, silver, cobalt, and rare earth elements, often in higher concentrations than natural ore. Yet a large share of discarded devices is never properly recovered. Part of the reason is technical: devices glued rather than screwed together are difficult to disassemble. Part of it is economic: recovering small quantities of metal from millions of scattered devices costs more than mining new material, at least when environmental damage is not counted in the price.

　　Governments have begun to respond through regulation. Some jurisdictions now require manufacturers to fund the collection and recycling of their own products, a policy known as extended producer responsibility. Others have introduced "right to repair" rules obliging companies to supply spare parts and repair manuals. Early evidence suggests such measures change design decisions — not because manufacturers become more idealistic, but because disposal costs finally appear on their own balance sheets.
107. What is the main difference between the linear and circular models?
(A) The linear model is more expensive.
(B) The circular model keeps materials in use rather than discarding them.
(C) The linear model uses only recycled materials.
(D) The circular model avoids manufacturing altogether.
108. According to the passage, why are many smartphones not properly recycled?
(A) They contain no valuable materials.
(B) Recycling is illegal in most countries.
(C) They are hard to disassemble and recovery is often uneconomical.
(D) Consumers are required to keep them.
109. What does "extended producer responsibility" require?
(A) Manufacturers must fund collection and recycling of their products.
(B) Consumers must pay a recycling fee.
(C) Governments must build recycling plants.
(D) Retailers must accept all returns.
110. The last sentence suggests that design changes occur mainly because
(A) manufacturers have become more ethical
(B) consumers demand it
(C) disposal costs now affect manufacturers financially
(D) recycling technology has improved
看詳解與全文中譯
📋 答案：107.(B)　108.(C)　109.(A)　110.(C)
題答案解析

主旨／對比題		(B)第 2 段：materials remain in circulation rather than becoming waste。
(D) 太極端——文章從未說「完全不製造」
108
細節題		(C)第 3 段給了兩個原因：技術上（glued rather than screwed... difficult to disassemble）與經濟上（costs more than mining new material）。
(A) 說反了——文章明說手機含金銀鈷等貴金屬
