📖 全文中譯 　　在工業時代的大部分時間裡，經濟一直沿著一條直線運行：開採原料、製造產品、使用，然後丟棄。這種「線性」模式假設資源是無限的，而廢棄物永遠可以被放到別的地方去。今天這兩個假設都不再成立。

　　循環經濟提出了另一種形狀。產品從一開始就被設計成可以維修、再使用、翻新，最後回收，讓材料持續留在循環中，而非變成廢棄物。這個想法並不新——維修店與二手市場已經存在數百年——但把它當成一項「設計原則」而非「事後補救」，卻是相當晚近的發展。

　　電子廢棄物提供了一個清楚的例子。一支現代智慧型手機含有金、銀、鈷與稀土元素，其濃度往往還高於天然礦石。然而大量遭丟棄的裝置從未被妥善回收。原因之一是技術性的：以膠黏合而非以螺絲鎖固的裝置，很難拆解。原因之二是經濟性的：從數以百萬計、散落各處的裝置中回收少量金屬，成本高於直接開採新料——至少在環境損害尚未被計入價格時是如此。

　　各國政府已開始以法規回應。有些法域現在要求製造商出資支應其自身產品的回收與處理，此一政策稱為「延伸生產者責任」。另有一些則推出「維修權」規定，課予企業提供備品與維修手冊的義務。初步證據顯示，這類措施確實改變了設計決策——並不是因為製造商變得更有理想，而是因為處理廢棄物的成本，終於出現在他們自己的資產負債表上。
📝 值得抄進單字本的字 extract 開採｜linear 線性的｜circular economy 循環經濟｜refurbish 翻新｜afterthought 事後補救｜ore 礦石｜disassemble 拆解｜jurisdiction 法域｜oblige 課予義務｜balance sheet 資產負債表
💡 這篇的高階解題技巧 看到 not because A, but because B 的句型，立刻在題目卷上圈起來。
它幾乎必然對應一道推論題，而 A 就是那個誘答選項。（本篇第 110 題完全符合。）
閱讀 6演算法與公平 · 111～114
　　Public agencies increasingly use automated systems to help make decisions: which applications to review first, which cases may involve fraud, which neighborhoods should receive additional inspections. Supporters argue that such systems are consistent, fast, and free from the moods and prejudices of individual officials.

　　That argument contains a hidden assumption. An algorithm learns from historical data, and historical data records past decisions — including biased ones. If a city inspected certain districts more often in the past, the resulting data will show more violations there, and a system trained on that data will recommend inspecting those districts even more. The bias is not introduced by the machine; it is inherited, and then amplified.

　　A second difficulty is explanation. When a citizen is denied a benefit, administrative law generally entitles them to know why. A simple rule can be stated in a sentence. A complex model may involve thousands of weighted factors that resist any short summary. Agencies that cannot explain a decision may find it difficult to defend in an appeal.

　　None of this means automation should be rejected. It means that automated systems in government require safeguards that commercial systems may not: documentation of the data used, regular testing for disparate impact across groups, a meaningful human review before adverse decisions take effect, and a clear record of who is accountable. Technology can assist judgment. It cannot assume responsibility.
111. What is the "hidden assumption" mentioned in the second paragraph?
(A) That algorithms are expensive　(B) That historical data is free from bias
(C) That officials are always fair　(D) That automation is slow
112. According to the passage, how does an algorithm amplify bias?
(A) By inventing new prejudices
(B) By learning from data that reflects past biased decisions
(C) By refusing to process certain applications
(D) By deleting historical records
113. Why is explanation a problem for complex models?
(A) They involve many weighted factors that are hard to summarize.
(B) They are protected by trade secrets.
(C) They never produce errors.
(D) They operate too slowly.
114. What does the final sentence mean?
(A) Technology should replace human officials.
(B) Humans remain responsible for decisions.
(C) Technology cannot assist decision-making.
(D) Responsibility should be assigned to the software vendor.
看詳解與全文中譯
📋 答案：111.(B)　112.(B)　113.(A)　114.(B)
題答案解析
111
細節／推論題		(B)第 2 段緊接著解釋：演算法從歷史資料學習，而歷史資料記錄了過去的決定，包括有偏誤的決定 → 隱含假設就是「歷史資料是中立的」
112
細節題		(B)原文：The bias is not introduced by the machine; it is inherited, and then amplified.
(A) 恰好是文章否定的（不是機器發明偏誤）
113
細節題		(A)第 3 段：thousands of weighted factors that resist any short summary
(B) 商業機密：文章沒提——聽起來合理但屬「文章未提及」的陷阱
114
字義／主旨題		(B)Technology can assist judgment. It cannot assume responsibility.（assume responsibility＝承擔責任）→ 責任仍在人。
(C) 說反了——文章明說技術「can assist」
📖 全文中譯 　　公務機關日益使用自動化系統協助決策：哪些申請案應優先審查、哪些案件可能涉及詐欺、哪些社區應加強稽查。支持者主張，這類系統一致、快速，且不受個別承辦人情緒與偏見的影響。

　　這項論點中藏有一個未被說出的假設。演算法從歷史資料中學習，而歷史資料記錄的是過去的決定——包括那些帶有偏誤的決定。如果某座城市過去比較常稽查特定行政區，所產生的資料就會顯示那裡有較多違規；而以該資料訓練出來的系統，便會建議更加頻繁地稽查那些行政區。偏誤並非由機器所引入，而是被繼承下來，然後被放大。

　　第二項困難在於「解釋」。當一位民眾的申請遭到駁回，行政法原則上賦予他知悉理由的權利。一條簡單的規則可以用一句話說清楚；而一個複雜的模型，可能牽涉數以千計的加權因子，難以化約為任何簡短的摘要。無法解釋決定理由的機關，在訴願程序中將難以為自己辯護。

　　以上種種並不意味著應該拒絕自動化。它意味著：政府部門的自動化系統，需要商業系統未必需要的保障機制——所用資料的文件紀錄、定期檢測其對不同群體是否造成差別影響、在不利決定生效前的實質人工覆核，以及明確記載由誰負責。科技可以輔助判斷，但它無法承擔責任。
📝 值得抄進單字本的字 prejudice 偏見｜bias 偏誤｜inherit 繼承｜amplify 放大｜entitle 賦予權利｜weighted factors 加權因子｜resist 難以（被…）｜safeguard 保障機制｜disparate impact 差別影響｜adverse decision 不利決定｜assume responsibility 承擔責任
💡 一魚三吃 這篇同時對應三個考科：
‧ 英文閱讀（4 分）　‧ 資訊管理申論（AI 治理、演算法偏誤）　‧ 國文作文（「人工智慧時代的人文價值」）
末句「科技可以輔助判斷，但無法承擔責任」——直接可以當作文的金句使用。
閱讀 7遠距醫療 · 115～118
　　Telemedicine — the delivery of medical care through video, telephone, or messaging — expanded dramatically when in-person visits became difficult. What began as an emergency measure has since become a permanent part of many health systems.

　　The advantages are clearest at the margins. A patient on an outlying island who once spent a full day traveling for a ten-minute consultation can now speak to a specialist from home. Follow-up appointments, medication reviews, and mental health counseling translate particularly well to remote formats, since they depend more on conversation than on physical examination.

　　The limits are equally clear. A physician cannot palpate an abdomen through a screen, and video quality varies. Studies have also found that telemedicine is used least by the populations that might benefit most: elderly patients, those with limited digital literacy, and households without reliable broadband. Without deliberate effort, a service intended to widen access can quietly narrow it.

　　Regulation has struggled to keep pace. Questions of licensing across jurisdictions, reimbursement rates, and the security of transmitted health records remain only partly settled. Health records are among the most sensitive categories of personal data, and a video platform adopted hastily during a crisis may not meet the standards that would be applied to a hospital information system. Most experts therefore argue that telemedicine should be treated not as a substitute for in-person care, but as one channel within a system that offers several.
115. Which type of care does the passage say suits telemedicine particularly well?
(A) Emergency surgery　(B) Physical examinations
(C) Mental health counseling　(D) Laboratory testing
116. What paradox does the third paragraph describe?
(A) Telemedicine is cheaper but less popular.
(B) Those who could benefit most use it least.
(C) Doctors prefer it but patients do not.
(D) It works better in cities than in hospitals.
117. What concern is raised about health records?
(A) They are too long.
(B) Platforms adopted hastily may not meet security standards.
(C) They cannot be stored digitally.
(D) Patients refuse to share them.
118. What is the conclusion of most experts?
(A) Telemedicine should replace in-person care.
(B) Telemedicine should be discontinued.
(C) Telemedicine should be one channel among several.
(D) Telemedicine should be limited to cities.
看詳解與全文中譯
📋 答案：115.(C)　116.(B)　117.(B)　118.(C)
題答案解析
115
細節題		(C)第 2 段：Follow-up appointments, medication reviews, and mental health counseling translate particularly well——選項中只有 (C) 在清單裡
116
細節／概念題		(B)第 3 段：telemedicine is used least by the populations that might benefit most——幾乎原句。末句「原本要擴大近用的服務，反而悄悄縮小了它」正是這個矛盾
117
細節題		(B)末段：a video platform adopted hastily during a crisis may not meet the standards
118
主旨／結論題		(C)末句：not as a substitute for in-person care, but as one channel within a system that offers several
(A) 正是文章明確否定的（not as a substitute）——又是一個 not...but... 句型的考點
📖 全文中譯 　　遠距醫療——透過視訊、電話或訊息提供醫療照護——在面對面就診變得困難時大幅擴張。原本作為緊急措施而起的做法，此後已成為許多醫療體系中的常設環節。

　　其優勢在邊緣地帶最為明顯。一位離島病患，過去為了十分鐘的看診得花上一整天路程，如今可以在家中與專科醫師對談。回診追蹤、用藥檢視與心理健康諮商特別適合轉為遠距形式，因為它們倚賴的是對話而非身體檢查。

　　其侷限同樣清楚。醫師無法隔著螢幕觸診腹部，而視訊品質也參差不齊。研究還發現，遠距醫療使用率最低的，恰恰是最可能從中受益的族群：年長病患、數位識讀能力有限者，以及沒有穩定寬頻的家戶。若不刻意投入努力，一項旨在擴大近用的服務，反而會悄悄地縮小它。

　　法規則一直難以跟上腳步。跨轄區的執業執照、給付費率，以及傳輸中健康紀錄的安全性等問題，都只獲得部分解決。健康紀錄屬於最敏感的個人資料類別之一，而危機期間倉促採用的視訊平臺，未必符合我們會對醫院資訊系統所要求的標準。因此多數專家主張：遠距醫療不應被視為面對面照護的替代品，而應被視為一個提供多種選項的體系中的其中一條管道。
📝 值得抄進單字本的字 telemedicine 遠距醫療｜in-person 面對面的｜outlying island 離島｜consultation 看診｜follow-up 回診追蹤｜palpate 觸診｜digital literacy 數位識讀能力｜broadband 寬頻｜reimbursement 給付｜hastily 倉促地｜substitute 替代品
💡 重複出現的主題模式 注意到了嗎？克漏字 1、閱讀 3、閱讀 7 都出現同一個論證結構：
「數位化很好 → 但會排除某些人 → 所以要保留非數位管道」
這是國考英文最常見的論證模式——認出它之後，主旨題與推論題幾乎不用細讀就能答對。
閱讀 8公務人力 · 119～124（6 題）
　　Recruiting and keeping skilled information technology staff has become one of the persistent difficulties facing public administration. The reason is not mysterious. A programmer with five years of experience can often earn substantially more in the private sector, and the gap tends to widen with seniority.

　　Salary, however, is only part of the story. Surveys of public sector technologists repeatedly identify other factors: outdated equipment, procurement rules that make it slow to obtain software, and organizational structures in which technical staff report to managers with no technical background. One respondent summarized the frustration in a single sentence: "It is not that I am paid less. It is that I am prevented from doing the work well."

　　Some agencies have found partial remedies. Rotation programs allow staff to move between units, reducing the sense of being trapped in a single maintenance role. Others have created technical career paths that permit promotion without moving into management — recognizing that a talented engineer does not necessarily become a talented administrator. A smaller number have negotiated flexible working arrangements, which surveys suggest are valued nearly as highly as pay among younger employees.

　　There are also advantages that the public sector should not undersell. Government systems affect millions of people, and the work is often visible in a way that internal corporate tools are not. Job security remains greater than in most private firms. And for a meaningful number of applicants, the opportunity to build something that serves the whole population — rather than a single company's customers — is itself a reason to apply. Agencies that fail to mention these points in recruitment are competing on the one dimension where they are least likely to win.
119. What is the main topic of this passage?
(A) How to design government IT systems
(B) Challenges in recruiting and retaining public sector IT staff
(C) Why private companies pay more
(D) The history of public administration
120. According to the quoted respondent, what is the main source of frustration?
(A) Low pay　(B) Long working hours
(C) Being prevented from doing the work well　(D) Lack of job security
121. Why were technical career paths created?
(A) To reduce salaries
(B) To allow promotion without moving into management
(C) To eliminate maintenance roles
(D) To increase working hours
122. What does the passage say about flexible working arrangements?
(A) They are unpopular.
(B) Younger employees value them nearly as highly as pay.
(C) They are illegal in the public sector.
(D) They reduce productivity.
123. The word "undersell" in the last paragraph most nearly means
(A) sell at a discount　(B) present as less valuable than it is
(C) advertise widely　(D) discontinue
124. What is the main point of the last sentence?
(A) Agencies should offer higher salaries.
(B) Agencies should emphasize their non-salary advantages.
(C) Agencies should stop recruiting IT staff.
(D) Agencies cannot compete with private firms at all.
看詳解與全文中譯
📋 答案：119.(B)　120.(C)　121.(B)　122.(B)　123.(B)　124.(B)
題答案解析
119
主旨題		(B)首句即點題：Recruiting and keeping skilled IT staff has become one of the persistent difficulties。
(C) 太窄（只是第 1 段的一個原因）
120
細節題		(C)引號原句："It is not that I am paid less. It is that I am prevented from doing the work well."
(A) 正是被明確否定的那一項（It is not that I am paid less）
121
細節題		(B)第 3 段：technical career paths that permit promotion without moving into management
122
細節題		(B)第 3 段末：valued nearly as highly as pay among younger employees
123
字義題		(B)undersell＝低估、未充分彰顯其價值。
解法：把選項代回原句——「政府部門不應低估的優勢」通順；「不應打折出售」不通。
字義題永遠看上下文，不要憑字面拆解（under + sell）
124
推論題		(B)末句：Agencies that fail to mention these points are competing on the one dimension where they are least likely to win（那個唯一的維度就是「薪水」）→ 意即應該強調薪資以外的優勢。
(D) 太極端——文章列了一整段公部門的優勢，並未說「完全無法競爭」
📖 全文中譯 　　招募並留住優秀的資訊技術人才，已成為公共行政所面臨的長期難題之一。原因並不難理解：一位有五年經驗的程式設計師，在民間部門往往能賺得多出許多，而且這個差距通常會隨著年資而擴大。

　　然而，薪資只是故事的一部分。針對公部門技術人員的調查一再指出其他因素：老舊的設備、使取得軟體變得曠日費時的採購規定，以及技術人員必須向毫無技術背景的主管報告的組織結構。一位受訪者用一句話總結了這份挫折：「問題不在於我領得比較少，而在於我被阻擋著、無法把工作做好。」

　　有些機關已找到部分的解方。輪調計畫讓人員得以在不同單位間移動，減輕那種被困在單一維運角色裡的感受。另有一些機關建立了技術職涯路徑，讓人員不必轉任管理職也能升遷——這等於承認：一位優秀的工程師，未必會成為一位優秀的行政管理者。還有為數較少的機關談成了彈性工作安排，而調查顯示，在年輕員工心目中，這項條件的價值幾乎與薪資相當。

　　公部門也有一些不該被低估的優勢。政府系統影響數以百萬計的人，而這份工作的成果往往是看得見的，不像企業內部工具那樣默默無聞。工作保障仍優於多數民間公司。而且對相當數量的應徵者而言，能夠打造一項服務全體國民——而非某一家公司客戶——的東西，本身就是一個投遞履歷的理由。未能在招募時提及這些的機關，等於是在他們最不可能獲勝的那一個維度上競爭。
📝 值得抄進單字本的字 recruit 招募｜retain 留任｜persistent 長期的｜seniority 年資｜procurement 採購｜respondent 受訪者｜rotation program 輪調計畫｜career path 職涯路徑｜flexible working arrangements 彈性工作安排｜job security 工作保障｜undersell 低估價值｜dimension 維度
💡 這篇講的就是你自己 這篇文章的主角，正是「考上資訊處理之後的你」。
文中提到的三個公部門優勢——影響數百萬人、成果看得見、服務全體國民——
可以直接搬進國文作文「我心目中的理想公務人員」或「談為民服務的真諦」。

E. 進階字彙（125～154 題）

125
The agency is required to ____ a record of all data access for auditing purposes.
(A) retain　(B) detain　(C) obtain　(D) contain
詳解
答案：(A) retain 保留
中譯：該機關須保留所有資料存取的紀錄以供稽核。

-tain 家族再複習：retain 保留｜detain 拘留｜obtain 取得｜contain 包含｜maintain 維護｜sustain 維持
搭配：retain records / data for X years——資料保存年限的標準說法。
126
Officials must remain ____ and avoid any conflict of interest.
(A) impartial　(B) impatient　(C) imperfect　(D) impossible
詳解
答案：(A) impartial 公正無私的
中譯：公務人員須保持公正，並避免任何利益衝突。

公務倫理必背組：impartial 公正｜integrity 廉正｜conflict of interest 利益衝突｜recusal 迴避｜transparency 透明
字根提示：im-（否定）＋ partial（偏袒的）＝ 不偏袒的——拆得開就猜得到。
127
The department will ____ the outdated system with a cloud-based platform.
(A) replace　(B) replay　(C) repay　(D) repair
詳解
答案：(A) replace 取代
中譯：該部門將以雲端平臺汰換老舊系統。

介系詞是常考點：replace A with B（以 B 取代 A）——不是 replace A by B（被動時才用 by）。
近義：substitute A for B（注意方向相反：以 A 代替 B）——這組最容易搞錯。
128
Due to the ____ nature of the information, access is limited to senior officials.
(A) sensible　(B) sensitive　(C) senseless　(D) sensory
詳解
答案：(B) sensitive 敏感的、機敏的
中譯：由於該資訊具機敏性質，僅限高階主管存取。

⚠️ sensitive vs sensible（每年必考的一組）
sensitive敏感的、機敏的（sensitive data 機敏資料）
sensible明智的、合理的（a sensible decision 明智的決定）
兩者長得很像，意思卻完全不同。
129
The new procedure will ____ the time required to process applications by half.
(A) expand　(B) extend　(C) reduce　(D) exceed
詳解
答案：(C) reduce 減少
中譯：新流程可將處理申請所需的時間縮短一半。

解題線索：by half（減半）→ 必然是往下的動詞，(A) 擴大、(B) 延長、(D) 超過全部方向錯誤。

「減少」家族：reduce、decrease、cut、lower、diminish、curtail
片語：reduce by half 減半｜reduce to half 減至一半（by 是「減掉的量」，to 是「減後的結果」）
130
The contract includes a clause on the ____ of intellectual property rights.
(A) ownership　(B) leadership　(C) membership　(D) partnership
詳解
答案：(A) ownership 所有權、歸屬
中譯：契約中包含智慧財產權歸屬的條款。

委外契約必備字：intellectual property rights 智慧財產權｜ownership 歸屬｜source code delivery 原始碼交付｜exit clause 退場條款｜vendor lock-in 廠商鎖定

這正是資訊管理委外考點的英文版。
131
All findings must be documented and reported ____.
(A) accordingly　(B) according　(C) accord　(D) accordance
詳解
答案：(A) accordingly 相應地、據此
中譯：所有查核發現均須記錄並據此陳報。

詞性判斷即可解：空格修飾動詞 reported → 需副詞 → 只有 (A) 是副詞。

同字根的三種用法要分清：
accordingly副詞：據此、因此
according to介系詞片語：根據（後接名詞）
in accordance with介系詞片語：依照（公文最高頻）
132
The system automatically generates a report at ____ intervals.
(A) regular　(B) regularly　(C) regulate　(D) regulation
詳解
答案：(A) regular 定期的
中譯：系統會定期自動產生報表。

詞性判斷：空格後是名詞 intervals → 需形容詞。
固定片語：at regular intervals（定期地）＝ regularly

其他 at + 名詞片語：at short notice 臨時通知｜at your earliest convenience 儘早（商用英文的客氣說法）｜at all times 隨時
133
The proposal was ____ approved by all members of the committee.
(A) unanimously　(B) uniformly　(C) universally　(D) uniquely
詳解
答案：(A) unanimously 全體一致地
中譯：該提案獲全體委員一致通過。

解題線索：by all members → 對應「全體一致」。
字根：un(i)-（一）＋ anim（心、精神）＝ 一條心

會議用語：unanimously 一致通過｜by majority 多數決｜abstain 棄權｜quorum 法定人數｜motion 動議｜minutes 會議紀錄
134
The audit revealed several ____ in the current control procedures.
(A) deficiencies　(B) efficiencies　(C) sufficiencies　(D) proficiencies
詳解
答案：(A) deficiencies 缺失、不足
中譯：稽核發現現行控管程序中存在若干缺失。

稽核英文：audit 稽核｜finding 查核發現｜deficiency 缺失｜corrective action 矯正措施｜follow-up 追蹤複查｜compliance 遵法

字首提示：de-（向下、不足） vs suf-/pro-（足夠、精通）——方向相反。
135
Employees who report misconduct are protected from ____ under the law.
(A) retaliation　(B) relaxation　(C) reputation　(D) reservation
詳解
答案：(A) retaliation 報復
中譯：依法舉報不當行為的員工受保護，免於遭受報復。

吹哨者保護的核心概念：whistleblower 吹哨者｜misconduct 不當行為｜retaliation 報復｜anonymity 匿名性｜protected disclosure 受保護之揭露

我國《公益揭弊者保護法》所處理的正是這個問題。
136
The city plans to ____ its emergency response capabilities before typhoon season.
(A) bolster　(B) bother　(C) border　(D) bounce
詳解
答案：(A) bolster 強化
中譯：該市計畫在颱風季前強化其緊急應變能量。

「強化」家族（閱讀測驗常出現）：bolster、strengthen、reinforce、enhance、fortify、shore up
其中 bolster 與 shore up 帶有「支撐住、避免衰退」的意味。
137
The policy has been criticized as ____ and difficult to enforce.
(A) ambiguous　(B) ambitious　(C) amphibious　(D) ambulance
詳解
答案：(A) ambiguous 含糊不清的
中譯：該政策因語意含糊且難以執行而遭批評。

解題線索：and difficult to enforce → and 連接的兩項應是同方向的負面評價，(B) ambitious（有野心的）語意不合。

法律與政策用語：ambiguous 含糊 ↔ unambiguous / explicit 明確｜vague 模糊｜clarify 釐清
這正好對應行政程序法第 5 條的「明確性原則」。
138
The two systems are not ____, so data cannot be transferred directly.
(A) comparable　(B) compatible　(C) competitive　(D) comprehensive
詳解
答案：(B) compatible 相容的
中譯：這兩套系統並不相容，因此資料無法直接轉移。

資訊必背：compatible 相容｜compatibility 相容性｜interoperability 互通性｜legacy system 舊系統｜migration 資料移轉

易混淆：(A) comparable 可相比的｜(C) competitive 有競爭力的｜(D) comprehensive 全面的
139
Failure to comply may ____ in a fine of up to NT$100,000.
(A) result　(B) cause　(C) lead　(D) bring
詳解
答案：(A) result（in）導致
中譯：未遵行者可能導致最高新臺幣 10 萬元之罰鍰。

看空格後的介系詞 in 就能解：
result in導致（後接結果）
lead to導致（(C) 要配 to）
cause / bring about直接接受詞，不用 in
法規英文常用句型：Failure to + V may result in + 處罰——「未…者，處…」的標準英譯。
140
The report provides a ____ overview of the current situation.
(A) concise　(B) precise　(C) conscious　(D) consensus
詳解
答案：(A) concise 簡明扼要的
中譯：該報告提供了現況的簡要概觀。

易混淆的 -cise / -cious：
concise簡潔的
precise精確的
conscious有意識的、察覺到的
consensus共識（名詞）
詞性提示：空格在 a ___ overview 之間 → 必是形容詞 → 立刻刪掉 (D)。
141
Applicants must submit documents ____ their identity and residence.
(A) verifying　(B) verified　(C) verify　(D) verification
詳解
答案：(A) verifying
中譯：申請人須繳交證明其身分與居住事實的文件。

文法點：分詞當形容詞修飾名詞
documents verifying ... ＝ documents which verify ...（主動：文件「證明」某事）

關係用法例
主動V-ingdocuments verifying identity（證明身分的文件）
被動p.p.documents submitted by the applicant（申請人所繳交的文件）
判準：名詞是「做」這個動作的 → V-ing；是「被做」的 → p.p.
142
The government has pledged to make the budgeting process more ____.
(A) transparent　(B) transient　(C) transferable　(D) translated
詳解
答案：(A) transparent 透明的
中譯：政府承諾使預算編製過程更加透明。

善治（good governance）四大關鍵字：transparency 透明｜accountability 課責｜participation 參與｜rule of law 法治
閱讀測驗只要談到政府治理，這四個字幾乎必定出現。

其他選項：(B) transient 短暫的｜(C) transferable 可移轉的｜(D) translated 翻譯的
143
The measure is intended to ____ the burden on small businesses.
(A) alleviate　(B) elevate　(C) allocate　(D) allege
詳解
答案：(A) alleviate 減輕
中譯：該措施旨在減輕小型企業的負擔。

搭配：alleviate the burden / poverty / pressure / suffering
反義：aggravate 加重、惡化

易混淆：(B) elevate 提升（方向相反）｜(C) allocate 分配｜(D) allege 宣稱
144
The commission was established to ____ complaints from the public.
(A) handle　(B) handful　(C) handy　(D) handling
詳解
答案：(A) handle 處理
中譯：該委員會設立之目的在處理民眾的申訴。

文法判斷：to ___ → 不定詞後接原形動詞。

「處理」家族：handle 處理｜address 處理（問題）｜deal with 應付｜tackle 著手解決｜process 辦理（案件）
address the issue 是最正式、最常出現在政策文件中的說法。
145
The plan is still ____ review and has not been finalized.
(A) under　(B) on　(C) at　(D) for
詳解
答案：(A) under 在…中
中譯：該計畫仍審查中，尚未定案。

under + 名詞＝「進行中」的固定用法：
under review審查中
under construction施工中
under investigation調查中
under consideration考慮中
under discussion討論中
under the law / under Article 5依法／依第 5 條
這一組在公文英文中出現頻率極高。
146
Officials are urged to ____ from accepting gifts from contractors.
(A) refrain　(B) retain　(C) restrain　(D) remain
詳解
答案：(A) refrain（from）避免、戒除
中譯：籲請公務人員避免接受承包商餽贈。

介系詞是解題關鍵：refrain from + V-ing（自我克制不做某事）

refrain from自制、避免（自己不做）
restrain約束、抑制（去限制別人或某物）
retain保留
remain維持、仍然是
相關公務倫理用語：prohibited from receiving gifts｜declare a conflict of interest 申報利益衝突｜recuse oneself 自行迴避
147
The findings are ____ with those of previous studies.
(A) consistent　(B) constant　(C) consequent　(D) content
詳解
答案：(A) consistent（with）一致的
中譯：本次研究發現與先前研究一致。

固定搭配：be consistent with（與…一致）
反義：be inconsistent with／contradict 牴觸

另一個意思：consistent 也可指「前後一貫的」——a consistent policy 前後一致的政策。
閱讀測驗中若問「研究結果如何」，consistent / contradict 這組字是判斷關鍵。
148
The service is available to all residents ____ of their income level.
(A) regardless　(B) regarding　(C) regard　(D) regards
詳解
答案：(A) regardless（of）無論、不論
中譯：本項服務開放所有居民使用，不論其所得高低。

⚠️ regardless of vs regarding（方向完全不同）
regardless of不論、不管（＝ irrespective of）
regarding關於（＝ concerning, with regard to）
兩者長得像，一個是「不管」、一個是「關於」，代進句子語意完全不同。
公文英文：with regard to your letter dated...（有關台端○月○日來函）——相當於中文公文的「復　台端…」。
149
The committee will convene ____ to review the progress of the project.
(A) quarterly　(B) quarter　(C) quantity　(D) quality
詳解
答案：(A) quarterly 每季地
中譯：委員會將每季召開會議以檢視專案進度。

頻率副詞整組背：
daily 每日weekly 每週biweekly 每兩週／每週兩次
monthly 每月quarterly 每季annually 每年
biannually 每年兩次biennially 每兩年periodically 定期
易錯提醒：biannual（一年兩次）與 biennial（兩年一次）只差一個字母，意思完全不同——公文中建議寫得更明確以免誤解。

另注意：convene（召開會議）是很正式的字，比 hold a meeting 更常見於公文。
150
Any personal data must be ____ before the dataset is made public.
(A) anonymized　(B) analyzed　(C) announced　(D) annotated
詳解
答案：(A) anonymized 匿名化
中譯：資料集公開前，任何個人資料均須經匿名化處理。

去識別化相關字（開放資料必考）：
anonymize匿名化（不可還原，脫離個資法適用）
pseudonymize假名化（仍可還原，仍屬個資）
de-identify去識別化
mask / redact遮罩
aggregate彙總（以總計數字取代個別紀錄）
re-identification重新識別（風險）
匿名化與假名化的差別，是法學知識與資安申論的共同考點。
151
The manual outlines the procedures to be followed ____ a system failure.
(A) in the event of　(B) in the case　(C) in event　(D) on event of
詳解
答案：(A) in the event of 萬一發生…時
中譯：該手冊列出系統故障時應遵循的程序。

應變文件的固定用語：
in the event of + N萬一發生…
in case of + N若遇…（注意沒有 the）
in the case of + N就…而言（意思不同！）
should + S + V倘若…（正式的假設語氣倒裝）
易錯：in case of（若遇）與 in the case of（就…而言）只差一個 the，意思完全不同——這是很刁鑽但確實會考的一組。
152
The agency's budget for next year has been ____ by 15 percent.
(A) slashed　(B) slipped　(C) slid　(D) sliced
詳解
答案：(A) slashed 大幅削減
中譯：該機關明年度預算已遭大幅削減 15%。

新聞英文常用的「削減」：slash（大幅砍）｜cut（削減）｜trim（小幅修剪）｜freeze（凍結）

閱讀測驗的用處：這些動詞帶有「幅度」與「語氣強度」的訊息——
看到 slash 就知道作者認為幅度很大，這對態度題很有幫助。
153
The two departments have overlapping responsibilities, which often ____ confusion.
(A) gives rise to　(B) gives up　(C) gives in　(D) gives out
詳解
答案：(A) gives rise to 引起、導致
中譯：兩個部門職掌重疊，這往往導致混淆。

give 的片語動詞整理：
give rise to導致、引起（＝ cause）
give up放棄
give in屈服、讓步
give out分發；耗盡
give way to讓路給；被…取代
公務情境字：overlapping responsibilities 職掌重疊｜jurisdictional dispute 權責爭議｜demarcation 權責劃分
正是公文範例 12（協調權責爭議）的英文版。
154
The regulation stipulates that records ____ for at least seven years.
(A) be retained　(B) are retained　(C) will retain　(D) retaining
詳解
答案：(A) be retained
中譯：該規定明訂紀錄應保存至少七年。

兩個文法點合併考：
① 假設語氣現在式stipulate / require / demand / recommend + that + S + 原形動詞（should 省略）
② 被動語態紀錄是「被保存」的 → be + p.p.
→ 合起來就是 (should) be retained。

與第 61、67 題同一規則——這條規則在國考英文出現頻率很高，且答對率偏低，記起來就是分數。

stipulate（明訂）是法規英文的高頻動詞，近義：provide、prescribe、specify。

F. 閱讀測驗（進階 4 篇，155～172 題）

閱讀 9政府採購與創新 · 155～158
　　Public procurement accounts for a substantial share of government spending, and in recent years officials have begun to ask whether that spending could do more than simply obtain goods at the lowest price.

　　The traditional model rewards the cheapest compliant bid. Its appeal is obvious: price is easy to measure, easy to compare, and easy to defend if questioned. But the same qualities that make it defensible also make it blunt. A contractor who wins by cutting labour costs may deliver a technically acceptable product while paying below-market wages, and a supplier offering the lowest initial price may impose far higher maintenance costs over a decade.

　　Several countries have therefore introduced criteria beyond price — environmental impact, employment of disadvantaged workers, or the total cost of ownership across a product's lifetime. Evidence suggests these criteria can shift supplier behaviour, since firms adapt to whatever the buyer actually measures. The difficulty is that broader criteria are harder to evaluate objectively, and every additional dimension of judgment creates room for dispute, and occasionally for favouritism.

　　Experienced practitioners tend to converge on a middle position. Rather than abandoning price, they recommend defining criteria precisely enough that two independent evaluators would reach similar conclusions, publishing the weighting in advance, and documenting the reasoning behind each score. The aim is not to make procurement subjective, but to make it measure the right things — and to leave a record that can withstand scrutiny.
155. What is the main advantage of the lowest-price model according to the passage?
(A) It produces the highest quality.　(B) It is easy to measure and to defend.
(C) It eliminates all disputes.　(D) It reduces maintenance costs.
156. The word "blunt" in the second paragraph most nearly means
(A) rude　(B) expensive　(C) lacking precision　(D) illegal
157. What difficulty does the passage identify with broader criteria?
(A) They are illegal in most countries.
(B) They are harder to evaluate objectively and may create room for dispute.
(C) Suppliers refuse to participate.
(D) They always increase costs.
158. What position do experienced practitioners take?
(A) Price should be the only criterion.
(B) Price should be abandoned entirely.
(C) Criteria should be defined precisely, published in advance, and documented.
(D) Procurement decisions should be made subjectively.
看詳解與全文中譯
📋 答案：155.(B)　156.(C)　157.(B)　158.(C)
題答案解析
155
細節題		(B)第 2 段：price is easy to measure, easy to compare, and easy to defend——幾乎原句
156
字義題		(C)blunt 字面是「鈍的」，此處引申為「粗糙、不夠精細」。
解法：看上下文——後面舉的例子（技術合格卻低薪、初價低卻維護費高）都在說「單看價格分辨不出好壞」→ 缺乏精確性。
(A) rude 是 blunt 的另一個常見意思（說話直率），但代回本句不通
157
細節題		(B)第 3 段末：harder to evaluate objectively, and every additional dimension of judgment creates room for dispute
158
細節／主旨題		(C)末段三項建議：defining criteria precisely、publishing the weighting in advance、documenting the reasoning。
(B) 是陷阱——原文明說 Rather than abandoning price（而非放棄價格）
📖 全文中譯 　　政府採購占政府支出相當大的比重，而近年來官員開始追問：這筆支出能否不只是「以最低價取得貨品」而已。

　　傳統模式獎勵的是符合規格中的最低標。它的吸引力顯而易見：價格易於衡量、易於比較，受到質疑時也容易辯護。但使它站得住腳的那些特質，同時也使它顯得粗糙。一家靠壓低人力成本得標的廠商，可能交出技術上合格的產品，卻支付低於市場水準的薪資；而提出最低初始價格的供應商，也可能在往後十年帶來高出許多的維護成本。

　　因此，若干國家已導入價格以外的評選標準——環境衝擊、雇用弱勢工作者，或產品生命週期的總持有成本。有證據顯示這些標準確實能改變供應商的行為，因為廠商會去適應買方真正衡量的東西。困難之處在於：較廣泛的標準更難客觀評量，而每增加一個判斷面向，就多出一分爭議的空間，偶爾也多出一分偏袒的空間。

　　經驗豐富的實務工作者傾向於一個折衷立場。他們建議的不是放棄價格，而是把標準定義得夠精確，使兩位獨立的評審會得出相近的結論；事先公布權重；並就每一項評分記載理由。其目的不是讓採購變得主觀，而是讓它衡量正確的事情——並留下一份經得起檢驗的紀錄。
📝 值得抄進單字本的字 procurement 採購｜substantial share 相當大的比重｜compliant bid 符合規格之投標｜blunt 粗糙的、鈍的｜impose costs 造成成本｜total cost of ownership 總持有成本｜weighting 權重｜favouritism 偏袒｜withstand scrutiny 經得起檢驗
💡 一魚兩吃 「最低標 vs 最有利標」「總持有成本」「評選標準應事先公布並記載理由」——
這正是資訊管理委外採購考點，也是公文範例 14（招標公告）提到的「不得有偏袒特定廠商之規格描述」。
閱讀 10遠端工作與管理 · 159～162
　　When remote work expanded rapidly, many managers assumed the central problem would be productivity. Would people actually work? Several years of data suggest that this was, for most organizations, the wrong question. Output generally held steady or improved, particularly for tasks requiring concentration.

　　The genuine difficulties emerged elsewhere, and more slowly. New employees, deprived of the incidental conversations that surround a desk, took considerably longer to understand how their organization actually worked — not the formal procedures, which are written down, but the informal knowledge of whom to ask and which rules bend. Managers who had relied on visible presence found they had no substitute for it, and some responded by installing monitoring software, a decision that researchers consistently associate with lower trust and higher turnover.

　　A further problem is that remote work is not distributed evenly. Roles that can be performed from anywhere tend to be office-based and relatively senior; those requiring physical presence — maintenance, front-desk service, inspection — cannot. Within a single organization, this can create two classes of employee with visibly different conditions, a source of resentment that few policies address directly.

　　None of this argues for a return to full-time office attendance, which surveys show most employees would resist. It argues instead for managing deliberately: defining which activities genuinely require co-location, investing in structured onboarding rather than hoping new staff absorb knowledge by proximity, and acknowledging openly that flexibility cannot be offered equally to everyone.
159. According to the passage, what did managers initially worry about?
(A) Employee turnover　(B) Productivity　(C) Office rental costs　(D) Data security
160. What difficulty did new employees face?
(A) They could not access formal procedures.
(B) They took longer to learn informal organizational knowledge.
(C) They were paid less than office workers.
(D) They lacked the necessary equipment.
161. What does the passage say about monitoring software?
(A) It improves productivity significantly.
(B) Researchers associate it with lower trust and higher turnover.
(C) It is required by law in most countries.
(D) It solves the onboarding problem.
162. What does the author ultimately recommend?
(A) Returning to full-time office attendance
(B) Eliminating remote work for senior staff
(C) Managing deliberately and acknowledging that flexibility is unequal
(D) Installing monitoring software in all departments
看詳解與全文中譯
📋 答案：159.(B)　160.(B)　161.(B)　162.(C)
題答案解析
159
細節題		(B)首段：many managers assumed the central problem would be productivity。
注意：作者接著說這是錯的問題，但題目問的是「當初擔心什麼」，答案仍是 productivity
160
細節題		(B)第 2 段：not the formal procedures, which are written down, but the informal knowledge。
(A) 恰是被否定的那一半——正式程序有書面，不是問題所在。又一個 not...but... 的考點
161
細節題		(B)第 2 段末：researchers consistently associate with lower trust and higher turnover
162
主旨／推論題		(C)末段三項建議，並明說 acknowledging openly that flexibility cannot be offered equally。
(A) 被明確否定：None of this argues for a return to full-time office attendance
📖 全文中譯 　　當遠端工作迅速擴張時，許多主管以為核心問題會是生產力：人們真的會工作嗎？數年的資料顯示，對多數組織而言，這問錯了問題。產出普遍持平或提升，對於需要專注的工作尤其如此。

　　真正的困難出現在別的地方，而且浮現得比較慢。新進員工失去了圍繞著辦公桌所發生的那些偶發對話，因而花費長得多的時間才理解組織實際上是怎麼運作的——不是那些寫在紙上的正式程序，而是「該問誰」「哪些規則有彈性」這類非正式的知識。過去倚賴「看得見人在」來管理的主管，發現自己找不到替代方式，其中有些人的因應之道是安裝監控軟體——研究一致指出，這項決定與信任降低及離職率上升相關聯。

　　另一個問題是，遠端工作的機會分配並不平均。能夠在任何地方執行的職務，往往是辦公室型且職級相對較高的；而那些需要實體到場的工作——維修、櫃檯服務、稽查——則不能。在同一個組織之內，這可能造就出條件明顯不同的兩類員工，成為一種怨懟的來源，而少有政策直接處理它。

　　以上種種，都不構成「應該回到全時進辦公室」的理由——調查顯示多數員工會抗拒這一點。它們主張的是刻意地去管理：界定哪些活動真正需要共處一地、投資於有結構的新人引導（而不是期待新人靠著待在附近自然吸收知識），並且坦白承認彈性無法平等地提供給每一個人。
📝 值得抄進單字本的字 hold steady 持平｜deprived of 被剝奪｜incidental 偶發的｜informal knowledge 非正式知識｜substitute 替代品｜turnover 離職率｜co-location 共處一地｜onboarding 新人引導｜proximity 鄰近｜resentment 怨懟
💡 注意這篇的論證結構 「大家以為問題是 A，其實真正的問題是 B」——這是國考英文閱讀非常常見的一種開頭。
認出它之後，你會知道：第一段提到的 A 幾乎一定是某道題的誘答選項。（本篇第 159、162 題都印證了這一點。）
閱讀 11食品安全治理 · 163～167（5 題）
　　Food safety regulation illustrates a problem common to many areas of public administration: the resources available for inspection are always smaller than the number of premises that could be inspected.

　　One response is random sampling. It is simple, difficult to manipulate, and treats all businesses equally. Yet it is also inefficient. If most establishments comply most of the time, random inspection spends the majority of its effort confirming that compliant businesses are still compliant.

　　The alternative is risk-based targeting, in which inspection frequency is determined by factors such as the type of food handled, the size of the operation, and the establishment's own history of violations. Where it has been adopted, detection rates per inspection have typically risen. But the approach carries two costs that are easy to overlook. First, businesses classified as low risk may be inspected so rarely that problems go undetected for years. Second, because the classification relies on historical records, an establishment that has never been closely examined may appear low risk simply because nothing has been found there.

　　The second problem deserves particular attention, since it applies far beyond food safety. Any system that allocates scrutiny according to past findings will tend to confirm its own assumptions: places that are watched generate records, and records justify further watching. Statisticians describe this as a feedback loop, and the standard remedy is to reserve a portion of inspections for randomly selected targets — accepting some loss of efficiency in exchange for a check on the model's blind spots.

　　The broader lesson is that no allocation rule is neutral. Choosing where to look is itself a policy decision, and it should be made explicitly, reviewed periodically, and documented well enough that someone outside the agency could understand why one restaurant was visited four times and another not at all.
163. What basic problem does the passage identify?
(A) Inspectors are poorly trained.
(B) Inspection resources are smaller than the number of premises.
(C) Food businesses rarely comply.
(D) Regulations are too strict.
164. What is the main weakness of random sampling?
(A) It can be manipulated.　(B) It treats businesses unequally.
(C) It spends most effort confirming compliance.　(D) It is expensive to administer.
165. According to the passage, why might an establishment appear low risk without actually being so?
(A) Because it bribed inspectors
(B) Because classification relies on historical records and it has never been examined closely
(C) Because it is very small
(D) Because the law exempts it
166. What remedy do statisticians recommend for the feedback loop?
(A) Abandoning risk-based targeting entirely
(B) Inspecting only high-risk premises
(C) Reserving a portion of inspections for randomly selected targets
(D) Increasing penalties for violations
167. What is the "broader lesson" of the final paragraph?
(A) Food safety is unimportant.
(B) Choosing where to look is itself a policy decision requiring transparency.
(C) Random inspection is always superior.
(D) Agencies should inspect every premise equally.
看詳解與全文中譯
📋 答案：163.(B)　164.(C)　165.(B)　166.(C)　167.(B)
題答案解析
163
細節題		(B)首段原句：resources available for inspection are always smaller than the number of premises
164
細節題		(C)第 2 段：spends the majority of its effort confirming that compliant businesses are still compliant。
(A)(B) 說反了——原文說隨機抽查難以被操縱且對所有業者一視同仁，那是它的優點
165
細節題		(B)第 3 段末：classification relies on historical records... may appear low risk simply because nothing has been found there
166
細節題		(C)第 4 段末：reserve a portion of inspections for randomly selected targets。
(A) 太極端——文章只主張「保留一部分」，並未主張全面放棄
167
主旨題		(B)末段：no allocation rule is neutral. Choosing where to look is itself a policy decision，並要求明示、定期檢討、記載理由
📖 全文中譯 　　食品安全管理凸顯了公共行政許多領域共通的一個問題：可供稽查的資源，永遠少於可以被稽查的場所數量。

　　一種因應方式是隨機抽查。它簡單、難以被操縱，且對所有業者一視同仁。然而它同時也缺乏效率：如果多數場所在多數時候都合規，隨機稽查就會把大部分心力，花在確認「合規的業者依然合規」這件事上。

　　另一種做法是以風險為基礎的鎖定稽查：稽查頻率取決於處理食品的種類、營業規模，以及該場所自身的違規紀錄等因素。在已採行之處，每次稽查的查獲率通常上升。但這種做法帶有兩項容易被忽略的代價。第一，被歸類為低風險的業者可能極少被稽查，以致問題數年未被發現。第二，由於分類倚賴歷史紀錄，一個從未被仔細檢查過的場所，可能僅僅因為「那裡什麼都沒查到過」而顯得低風險。

　　第二個問題特別值得注意，因為它的適用範圍遠遠超出食品安全。任何依照過去發現來分配稽查密度的系統，都會傾向於印證它自己的假設：被盯著的地方會產生紀錄，而紀錄又正當化了進一步的盯查。統計學家稱此為回饋迴路，而標準的解方是：保留一定比例的稽查名額給隨機選取的對象——以犧牲部分效率，換取對模型盲點的一道檢查。

　　更廣泛的啟示是：沒有任何一種分配規則是中立的。「決定要看哪裡」本身就是一項政策決定，因此它應當被明示地作成、定期檢討，並記載得夠清楚，使機關以外的人也能理解：為什麼某一家餐廳被稽查了四次，另一家卻一次也沒有。
📝 值得抄進單字本的字 premises 場所｜random sampling 隨機抽查｜manipulate 操縱｜compliant 合規的｜risk-based targeting 風險導向鎖定｜detection rate 查獲率｜overlook 忽略｜feedback loop 回饋迴路｜blind spot 盲點｜allocation rule 分配規則
💡 這篇的觀念，你在專業科目也會用到 「回饋迴路使系統印證自己的假設」——這正是閱讀 6（演算法與公平）所講的演算法偏誤：過去多稽查某區 → 該區紀錄多 → 系統建議更常稽查該區。
同一個概念，換了一個題材再考一次——這也說明為什麼認出「論證結構」比認得每個單字更重要。
閱讀 12語言與公共服務 · 168～172（5 題）
　　Every public notice makes an assumption about its reader. Most assume a reader who is literate, unhurried, and familiar with administrative vocabulary. In practice, the people who most need to understand a notice are often the least likely to match that description.

　　The plain language movement began from this observation. Its argument is not that complex ideas should be avoided, but that unnecessary complexity should be. A sentence such as "Applicants failing to submit the requisite documentation within the aforementioned period shall be deemed to have withdrawn their application" contains no difficult concept. It is merely written in a register that many readers must decode rather than simply read.

　　Rewriting it — "If we do not receive your documents by 30 June, we will treat your application as cancelled" — takes little effort and produces measurable results. Trials in several countries have found that plain-language versions of official letters increase correct responses and reduce the volume of clarifying phone calls, which in turn saves staff time. The savings are frequently larger than the cost of the rewriting.

　　Resistance nonetheless persists, and it is worth understanding why. Some officials associate simplified language with reduced legal precision, a concern that is occasionally justified and more often not. Others feel, without quite saying so, that formal language signals seriousness and authority. And a genuine difficulty exists where a notice serves two audiences at once: the citizen who must act on it, and the court that may later review it.

　　The most successful programmes have handled that tension by separating the functions rather than compromising between them — stating the essential message in plain terms at the top of the document, and placing precise legal wording in a clearly marked section below. The reader who needs to act reads the first part. The reader who needs the exact provision finds it in the second. Neither is served by a document that tries to be both at once, and succeeds at neither.
168. What assumption do most public notices make?
(A) That readers have legal training
(B) That readers are literate, unhurried, and familiar with administrative vocabulary
(C) That readers will consult a lawyer
(D) That readers prefer long sentences
169. According to the passage, what is the plain language movement's actual argument?
(A) Complex ideas should be avoided.
(B) Unnecessary complexity should be avoided.
(C) All legal terms should be deleted.
(D) Notices should be shorter than 50 words.
170. What benefits did trials find?
(A) Higher printing costs　(B) More correct responses and fewer clarifying calls
(C) Longer processing times　(D) Reduced legal accuracy
171. What genuine difficulty does the passage acknowledge?
(A) Plain language is always less precise.
(B) A notice may serve both a citizen and a reviewing court.
(C) Officials cannot write clearly.
(D) Citizens do not read notices at all.
172. How do the most successful programmes resolve the tension?
(A) By compromising between the two styles
(B) By abandoning plain language
(C) By separating the plain message and the legal wording into distinct sections
(D) By producing two entirely separate documents
看詳解與全文中譯
📋 答案：168.(B)　169.(B)　170.(B)　171.(B)　172.(C)
題答案解析
168
細節題		(B)首段：a reader who is literate, unhurried, and familiar with administrative vocabulary——三個形容詞原句照抄
169
細節題		(B)第 2 段：not that complex ideas should be avoided, but that unnecessary complexity should be。
又是 not...but... 句型——(A) 正是被否定的那一半
170
細節題		(B)第 3 段：increase correct responses and reduce the volume of clarifying phone calls
171
細節題		(B)第 4 段末：a notice serves two audiences at once: the citizen who must act on it, and the court that may later review it。
(A) 說得太絕對——原文說這個疑慮 occasionally justified and more often not（偶爾成立，多數時候不成立）
172
細節／推論題		(C)末段：separating the functions rather than compromising between them——白話文在上、法律用語在下方明確標示的區塊。
(A) 正是被否定的（rather than compromising）；(D) 過度延伸——是同一份文件的兩個區塊，不是兩份文件
📖 全文中譯 　　每一份公告都對它的讀者做了某種假設。多數公告假設的讀者是識字的、不趕時間的，且熟悉行政用語的。而實際上，最需要看懂那份公告的人，往往最不符合這個描述。

　　白話文運動正是從這項觀察出發。它的主張並不是「複雜的概念應該被避免」，而是「不必要的複雜應該被避免」。像「申請人未於前揭期限內檢具應備文件者，視為撤回其申請」這樣一個句子，其中並不包含任何困難的概念；它只是被寫成了一種語域——許多讀者必須「解碼」，而非直接「閱讀」。

　　把它改寫成——「如果我們在 6 月 30 日前沒有收到您的文件，我們會把您的申請視為取消」——所費的力氣很小，產生的效果卻可以量測。若干國家的試辦發現，官方信函的白話版本能提高正確回應率、減少來電詢問的數量，進而節省了人員的時間。所節省的，經常大於改寫所付出的成本。

　　儘管如此，抗拒仍然存在，而理解其原因是值得的。有些官員把簡化的語言與法律精確性的降低聯想在一起——這個疑慮偶爾成立，但更多時候並不成立。另一些人則隱隱覺得（雖未明說），正式的語言傳達出嚴肅與權威。而確實存在一項真正的困難：一份公告往往同時服務兩種讀者——必須據以行動的民眾，以及日後可能加以審查的法院。

　　最成功的方案處理這項張力的方式，是把兩種功能「分開」，而不是在兩者之間「折衷」——在文件最上方以白話陳述核心訊息，並在下方一個清楚標示的區塊放置精確的法律文字。需要採取行動的讀者看第一部分；需要確切條文的讀者在第二部分找到它。一份試圖同時兼顧兩者、結果兩者皆未達成的文件，對誰都沒有幫助。
📝 值得抄進單字本的字 literate 識字的｜unhurried 不趕時間的｜requisite 必需的｜aforementioned 前揭的｜be deemed to 視為｜register（語言的）語域｜decode 解碼｜measurable 可量測的｜persist 持續存在｜tension 張力｜provision 條文
💡 這篇直接對應你的公文寫作 《文書處理手冊》要求公文應「簡、淺、明、確」——這正是本文所講的 plain language 原則的中文版。

文中「白話在上、法律文字在下」的做法，也對應了公文範例 5（駁回函）的結構：主旨用一句白話講結論，說明才引法條、最後附教示條款。

另外注意 aforementioned（前揭）這個字——它就是公文裡「旨揭」「前開」的英文對應。

G. 進階文法（173～202 題）

173時態
The committee ____ the proposal when the power suddenly went out.
(A) discussed　(B) was discussing　(C) has discussed　(D) discusses
詳解
答案：(B) was discussing
中譯：停電突然發生時，委員會正在討論該提案。

規則：兩個過去動作，「長的」用過去進行式，「短的」用過去簡單式。
━━━ was discussing（持續中）━━━
　　　　　　　　↓ went out（突然發生）
常見句型：S + was/were V-ing + when + S + 過去式｜While + S + was V-ing, S + 過去式
174被動
The regulations ____ to reflect recent technological changes.
(A) have revised　(B) have been revised　(C) revising　(D) revise
詳解
答案：(B) have been revised
中譯：這些法規已被修正，以反映近期的技術變化。

兩個判斷：① 法規是「被」修正的 → 被動；② 「已經」修正 → 現在完成式
→ have been + p.p.

各時態被動一覽：
現在is/are + p.p.
過去was/were + p.p.
現在完成have/has been + p.p.
未來will be + p.p.
情態must/should be + p.p.
175關係子句
The official ____ signature appears on the document has since retired.
(A) who　(B) whom　(C) whose　(D) which
詳解
答案：(C) whose
中譯：文件上簽名的那位官員此後已退休。

判斷：空格後緊接名詞（signature）→ 需要所有格關係代名詞 → whose。
　　（＝ the official's signature）

空格後面是用
動詞who / which（當主詞）
主詞＋動詞（缺受詞）whom / which（當受詞）
名詞whose（所有格）
完整句子where / when / why（關係副詞）
注意：whose 人與物皆可用——the building whose roof collapsed 也正確。
176句構
____ the system was upgraded, response times have improved significantly.
(A) Since　(B) Despite　(C) However　(D) Because of
詳解
答案：(A) Since
中譯：自從系統升級以來，回應時間已顯著改善。

兩層判斷：
① 空格後是完整子句 → 需連接詞 → 刪 (B) Despite、(D) Because of（皆為介系詞）
② (C) however 是副詞，不能連接兩個子句

⚠️ since 的兩個意思
自從主要子句常用完成式（本題 have improved）
因為＝ because，語氣較弱
看主要子句是不是完成式，就能分辨。
177動狀詞
The manager insisted on ____ the report before the deadline.
(A) submit　(B) to submit　(C) submitting　(D) submitted
詳解
答案：(C) submitting
中譯：主管堅持要在期限前繳交報告。

規則：介系詞後面一律接「名詞或動名詞」——on 是介系詞 → 接 V-ing。

易錯的「介系詞 + V-ing」意思
insist on V-ing堅持
look forward to V-ing期待（to 是介系詞，不是不定詞）
be used to V-ing習慣於（對照 used to V＝過去曾經）
object to V-ing反對
be committed to V-ing致力於
in addition to V-ing除了…之外
這一組「to + V-ing」是國考英文最愛考的陷阱——看到 to 就填原形是最常見的錯誤。
178比較
This year's budget is ____ than last year's.
(A) more high　(B) higher　(C) highest　(D) high
詳解
答案：(B) higher
中譯：今年的預算比去年高。

規則：than 前面必用比較級；且 high 是單音節形容詞 → 加 -er，不用 more。

音節比較級例
單音節-erhigh → higher｜fast → faster
雙音節（-y 結尾）去 y 加 -iereasy → easier
三音節以上more +efficient → more efficient
不規則—good→better｜bad→worse｜little→less｜many/much→more
179語態
A new policy on remote work ____ next month.
(A) will announce　(B) will be announced　(C) announces　(D) announcing
詳解
答案：(B) will be announced
中譯：關於遠距工作的新政策將於下個月公布。

判斷：政策不會自己宣布，是被公布的 → 被動；next month → 未來式。

💡 公文與新聞英文為何大量使用被動
① 重點在「事情」而非「誰做的」——The report was submitted.
② 刻意不指明行為人——Mistakes were made.（錯誤發生了，但不說是誰造成的）

閱讀測驗中，若整段大量使用被動且不指明主體，往往值得注意作者的態度。
180代名詞
Neither of the two proposals ____ the committee's requirements.
(A) meet　(B) meets　(C) are meeting　(D) have met
詳解
答案：(B) meets
中譯：這兩份提案都不符合委員會的要求。

規則：neither of／either of／each of 後面雖接複數名詞，但主詞是 neither/either/each 本身，視為單數。

⚠️ 三組容易搞混的一致性規則
neither of / either of / each of單數
neither A nor B／either A or B就近原則（看 B）
both of複數
對照第 70 題（neither...nor 用就近原則）——同樣是 neither，用法完全不同。
181分詞
The measures ____ last year have proven effective.
(A) introducing　(B) introduced　(C) introduce　(D) to introduce
詳解
答案：(B) introduced
中譯：去年推出的那些措施已證實有效。

判斷：措施是被推出的 → 過去分詞（p.p.）。
　　The measures which were introduced last year → 省略成 measures introduced last year

關係用例
名詞「做」動作V-ingthe officer handling the case（承辦此案的官員）
名詞「被」做p.p.the case handled by the officer（被該官員處理的案件）
解題訣竅：把它還原成關係子句，看該用主動還是被動。
182名詞
The agency provided several ____ of advice to the applicants.
(A) piece　(B) pieces　(C) advices　(D) advice
詳解
答案：(B) pieces
中譯：該機關向申請人提供了幾項建議。

兩個知識點：
① advice 是不可數名詞（沒有 advices）
② 要計數須用量詞：a piece of advice／several pieces of advice

不可數名詞量詞
advice、information、news、evidencea piece of
equipment、furniturean item of / a piece of
research、worka piece of
paper（紙）a sheet of
注意：paper 當「論文、報告」時可數（three papers）——同一個字，意思不同、可數性也不同。
183句構
It is important ____ all staff to complete the training.
(A) of　(B) for　(C) to　(D) with
詳解
答案：(B) for
中譯：全體同仁完成訓練是重要的。

規則：It is + 形容詞 + for + 人 + to V——形容詞描述「事情」時用 for。

⚠️ for 與 of 的區別（高頻考點）
介系詞形容詞類型例
for描述事情：important, necessary, difficult, easy, possibleIt is necessary for you to attend.
of描述人的品格：kind, nice, careless, wise, foolishIt is kind of you to help.
判斷法：把句子改成「某人是…的」看通不通。
「You are kind.」通 → 用 of；「You are important to attend.」不通 → 用 for。
184連接詞
The project was delayed ____ the lack of funding.
(A) because　(B) since　(C) owing to　(D) as
詳解
答案：(C) owing to
中譯：該專案因缺乏經費而延宕。

判斷：空格後是名詞片語（the lack of funding）→ 需介系詞。
(A)(B)(D) 都是連接詞，後面必須接完整子句。

後接名詞（介系詞）後接子句（連接詞）
owing to、due to、because of、on account of、thanks tobecause、since、as、now that
解題機械動作：先看空格後面第一個字——是名詞就找介系詞，是主詞就找連接詞。這個動作能解掉一半的連接詞題。
185語氣
I wish I ____ more time to review the document yesterday.
(A) have　(B) had　(C) had had　(D) will have
詳解
答案：(C) had had
中譯：真希望我昨天當時有更多時間審閱那份文件。

規則：wish 後面的子句用「比實際時間退一步」的時態。
希望的內容用例
與現在事實相反過去式I wish I had more time.（現在沒時間）
與過去事實相反had + p.p.I wish I had had more time.（當時沒時間）
對未來的期望would + 原形I wish it would stop raining.
本題有 yesterday → 與過去事實相反 → had had（看起來奇怪，但正確）。
186句構
So complex ____ that additional training was required.
(A) the system was　(B) was the system　(C) the system is　(D) is the system
詳解
答案：(B) was the system
中譯：這套系統如此複雜，以致於需要額外的訓練。

規則：So + 形容詞 置於句首時必須倒裝（be 動詞或助動詞提到主詞前）。
　　So complex was the system that...（＝ The system was so complex that...）

需倒裝的句首例
So + adj/advSo complex was the system that...
Such + NSuch was the demand that...
Only when / Only afterOnly then did we realize...
否定副詞（Never, Seldom, Rarely, Hardly, Not only）Never have we seen...
時態線索：後半句 was required 是過去式 → 前半也用 was。
187冠詞
The applicant submitted ____ application that was later approved.
(A) a　(B) an　(C) the　(D) 不填
詳解
答案：(B) an
中譯：申請人繳交了一份後來獲准的申請。

規則：a/an 依「發音」而非「字母」決定——application 以母音 /æ/ 開頭 → 用 an。

⚠️ 看發音不看字母的經典例子
用 an（母音開頭）用 a（子音開頭）
an hour（h 不發音）
an honest man
an MOU（唸 em）		a university（唸 /juː/）
a European
a one-way street（唸 /wʌn/）
為何不用 the：後面的關係子句只是補充說明，並未使其成為「特指的那一份」；且首次提及用 a/an。
188動狀詞
The agency stopped ____ paper forms after the system went online.
(A) to use　(B) using　(C) use　(D) used
詳解
答案：(B) using
中譯：系統上線後，該機關停止使用紙本表單。

⚠️ stop 的兩種用法，意思完全不同
stop V-ing停止做某事（不做了）
stop to V停下來去做某事（為了做而停）
例：He stopped smoking.（戒菸了）vs He stopped to smoke.（停下來抽菸）
同類「兩種接法、兩種意思」的動詞：
remember V-ing記得做過remember to V記得要去做
forget V-ing忘了做過forget to V忘了要去做
try V-ing試試看某方法try to V努力去做
regret V-ing後悔做過regret to V遺憾地要說
通則：V-ing 指向「已發生」，to V 指向「還沒發生」。
189句構
The reason ____ the system failed was a configuration error.
(A) which　(B) why　(C) what　(D) how
詳解
答案：(B) why
中譯：系統失效的原因是一項組態設定錯誤。

判斷：先行詞是 the reason（原因）→ 用關係副詞 why；且後面 the system failed 是完整子句。

先行詞關係副詞可替換為
the place / the officewherein which / at which
the time / the daywhenon which / at which
the reasonwhyfor which
the wayhow（the way 與 how 不可並用）in which
易錯提醒：the way how 是錯的——只能寫 the way 或 how，擇一。
190一致性
Statistics ____ a required course for students in this program.
(A) are　(B) is　(C) were　(D) have been
詳解
答案：(B) is
中譯：統計學是本學程學生的必修課。

規則：statistics 當「統計學」（學科）時單數；當「統計數據」時複數。
本題後面是 a required course（一門課）→ 指學科 → 單數。

字單數用法複數用法
statistics統計學（學科）統計數據
politics / economics / physics學科，恆單數—
means方法（單複數同形）方法們
data實務上常作單數正式寫作作複數（datum 的複數）
對照：The statistics show a rise in cases.（統計數據顯示…→ 複數）
191介系詞
The new rules will take effect ____ January 1, 2027.
(A) in　(B) at　(C) on　(D) by
詳解
答案：(C) on
中譯：新規定將於 2027 年 1 月 1 日生效。

時間介系詞三分法（必背）：
介系詞用於例
at時刻（點）at 3 p.m.、at noon、at midnight
on特定日期、星期（面）on January 1、on Monday、on that day
in月、季、年、世紀（範圍）in January、in 2027、in the morning
by不遲於（期限）by January 1（1 月 1 日前）
公文常見：effective on / effective from + 日期——正是中文公文「自○年○月○日生效」的英文。
192動詞
The director had the technician ____ the equipment before the inspection.
(A) checked　(B) to check　(C) check　(D) checking
詳解
答案：(C) check
中譯：主任在稽查前要技術人員檢查設備。

規則：使役動詞 have / make / let 後面接原形動詞（受詞主動做動作時）。

句型意思例
have + 人 + 原形要某人做（人主動做）have him check
have + 物 + p.p.使某物被做have the equipment checked
make / let + 人 + 原形使／讓make him go
get + 人 + to V要某人做（get 例外，要加 to）get him to check
判斷：本題受詞是 the technician（人，主動檢查）→ 原形。
若寫 had the equipment checked（物，被檢查）也對，但受詞不同。
193句構
____ is essential is that all data be encrypted.
(A) That　(B) What　(C) Which　(D) It
詳解
答案：(B) What
中譯：重要的是所有資料都必須加密。

規則：What ＝ the thing which，本身包含先行詞，可引導名詞子句當主詞。
　　What is essential is that...（重要的那件事是…）

選項錯在哪
(A) Thatthat 引導名詞子句時，後面須接完整句；That is essential 語意不完整
(C) Whichwhich 須有先行詞，句首無先行詞
(D) It會變成兩個句子沒有連接詞
另注意句尾：that all data be encrypted——essential 觸發假設語氣，用原形動詞（與第 67、154 題同一規則）。
194副詞
The department has ____ completed the migration to the new platform.
(A) near　(B) nearly　(C) nearby　(D) nearest
詳解
答案：(B) nearly 幾乎
中譯：該部門幾乎已完成遷移至新平臺的作業。

判斷：修飾動詞 completed → 需副詞；且語意為「幾乎」。

⚠️ 一組長得像、意思差很多的字
near形容詞／介系詞：接近的、在…附近
nearly副詞：幾乎（＝almost）
nearby形容詞／副詞：附近的、在附近
同類陷阱：hard（努力地）vs hardly（幾乎不）；late（晚）vs lately（最近）；high（高）vs highly（非常）。
這幾組「加 ly 意思就變了」的字，是字彙與文法題共同的高頻考點。
195時態
By the time the audit began, the department ____ all required documents.
(A) prepares　(B) prepared　(C) had prepared　(D) will prepare
詳解
答案：(C) had prepared
中譯：稽核開始時，該部門已經備妥所有應備文件。

規則：By the time + 過去式，主要子句用過去完成式（過去的過去）。
had prepared（先） ────► the audit began（後） ────► 現在
By the time + 時態主要子句
過去式過去完成式（had p.p.）
現在式（指未來）未來完成式（will have p.p.）
對照第 52 題（By the end of next year → will have migrated）——同一個 by，因時間點不同而用不同的完成式。
196語態
The incident report ____ to contain several inaccuracies.
(A) found　(B) was found　(C) finding　(D) finds
詳解
答案：(B) was found
中譯：該事件報告被發現含有若干不正確之處。

判斷：報告是「被發現」的 → 被動。
　　句型：S + be + p.p. + to V（被認為／被發現…）

公文與新聞高頻句型意思
be found to V被發現…
be considered to V被認為…
be believed to V據信…
be expected to V預期…
be said to V據說…
be required to V須…
💡 閱讀測驗的用處
be believed to、be said to 代表作者未親自證實——推論題若選項把這類敘述當成確定事實，通常是錯的。
197連接詞
The system is secure; ____, regular audits are still necessary.
(A) therefore　(B) nonetheless　(C) for instance　(D) similarly
詳解
答案：(B) nonetheless 儘管如此
中譯：系統是安全的；儘管如此，定期稽核仍屬必要。

邏輯判斷：前句正面（安全）、後句仍要求動作（還是要稽核）→ 轉折。

轉折詞語氣強度
however中性，最常用
nonetheless / nevertheless「儘管如此仍然…」——承認前句成立，但結論不變
on the contrary「正好相反」——否定前句
in contrast對比兩件事
細微但重要的差別：nonetheless 不否定前句（系統確實安全），只是說結論不受影響；on the contrary 則是直接推翻前句——選錯會使語意完全相反。
198句構
Not until the final review ____ the error discovered.
(A) was　(B) it was　(C) that was　(D) had
詳解
答案：(A) was
中譯：直到最後一次審查，才發現這項錯誤。

規則：Not until... 置於句首，主要子句須倒裝。
　　Not until the final review was the error discovered.
　　（＝ The error was not discovered until the final review.）

💡 倒裝的關鍵：倒裝的是「主要子句」，不是 not until 後面的那個子句
Not until he arrived（不倒裝）did we begin（倒裝）the meeting.

這是倒裝題最常見的錯誤——把錯的那半倒裝了。
同類句型：Only after...、Only when...、No sooner... than...
199動狀詞
The agency recommends ____ passwords every three months.
(A) to change　(B) changing　(C) change　(D) changed
詳解
答案：(B) changing
中譯：該機關建議每三個月更換密碼一次。

規則：recommend 後面接 V-ing 或 that 子句 + 原形動詞，不接 to V。
　　✔ recommends changing passwords
　　✔ recommends that passwords be changed（假設語氣）
　　✘ recommends to change

同樣「不接 to V」的動詞
recommend、suggest、advise、consider、admit、deny、avoid、mind、enjoy、finish、practice
對照第 61 題（suggested that the deadline be extended）——同一個動詞的兩種正確接法。
200綜合
Had the backup been stored offline, the data ____.
(A) will be recovered　(B) could have been recovered　(C) is recovered　(D) recovers
詳解
答案：(B) could have been recovered
中譯：假如當初備份有離線保存，資料本來是可以還原的。

兩個知識點合併：
① 假設語氣倒裝Had + S + p.p. ＝ If + S + had + p.p.（省略 if 並倒裝）
② 與過去事實相反主要子句用 would/could/might + have + p.p.；且資料是「被還原」→ 被動
→ could have been recovered

三種假設語氣的倒裝形式（省略 if）：
Were I you, ...＝ If I were you
Had I known, ...＝ If I had known
Should you need help, ...＝ If you should need help
💡 這題的內容你太熟了 「備份沒有離線保存，所以資料救不回來」——正是資安申論與克漏字 2反覆強調的那件事。
201代名詞
The two departments must coordinate with ____ to avoid duplication.
(A) each other　(B) themselves　(C) one another's　(D) other
詳解
答案：(A) each other
中譯：兩個部門必須彼此協調，以避免重複作業。

用法意思適用
each other彼此（互相）傳統上用於兩者
one another彼此傳統上用於三者以上（現代英語兩者常互換）
themselves他們自己（反身）動作回到自己身上
關鍵區別：coordinate with each other＝A 跟 B 協調；coordinate with themselves＝各自跟自己協調（語意不通）。

公務高頻詞組：coordinate with 協調｜duplication of effort 重複作業｜overlapping responsibilities 職掌重疊
202綜合
All personnel, ____ rank, must complete the annual security training.
(A) regardless of　(B) in spite　(C) despite of　(D) although
詳解
答案：(A) regardless of
中譯：全體人員，不論職級，均須完成年度資安訓練。

選項正誤說明
(A) regardless of✔後接名詞，意為「不論」
(B) in spite✘必須寫成 in spite of，少了 of 是錯的
(C) despite of✘despite 後面不加 of——這是最常見的錯誤之一
(D) although✘連接詞，後面須接完整子句
⚠️ 三個一定要記對的形式
✔ despite + N（沒有 of）｜✔ in spite of + N（有 of）｜✔ regardless of + N（有 of）
(B)(C) 正好是把 of 加錯位置的兩種典型錯誤。

H. 閱讀測驗（再進階 4 篇，203～220 題）

閱讀 13公務人力培育 · 203～207（5 題）
　　Most organizations describe training as an investment, and most measure it as an expense. The gap between those two statements explains a good deal about why training programmes so often disappoint.

　　Consider how the typical programme is evaluated. Attendance is recorded, a satisfaction form is distributed at the end of the session, and the results are reported as a percentage. Both measures are easy to collect and neither tells us whether anyone works differently afterwards. A course can be enjoyable, well attended, and entirely without effect.

　　Research on workplace learning consistently identifies a different factor as decisive: what happens in the weeks following the training. Skills that are not used within a short period are largely lost, and the strongest predictor of whether they are used is whether the participant's immediate supervisor expects them to be. A manager who asks, two weeks later, how the new method is working converts a course into a practice. A manager who never mentions it converts it into a memory.

　　This finding is inconvenient, because it locates responsibility somewhere other than the training department. It suggests that improving training may have less to do with better courses than with preparing supervisors — informing them beforehand what their staff will learn, and giving them a reason to follow up afterwards.

　　There is a further implication. If application depends on opportunity, then sending someone to a course they cannot use is worse than sending no one: it consumes budget, occupies time, and teaches participants that training is something to be endured rather than applied. Selecting the right participants is therefore not an administrative detail but a substantive decision — one that deserves as much attention as the choice of course itself.
203. What gap does the first paragraph identify?
(A) Between attendance and satisfaction
(B) Between describing training as an investment and measuring it as an expense
(C) Between managers and staff
(D) Between public and private sectors
204. According to the passage, what do attendance and satisfaction forms fail to show?
(A) How many people attended
(B) Whether participants enjoyed the course
(C) Whether anyone works differently afterwards
(D) How much the course cost
205. What does research identify as the decisive factor?
(A) The quality of the instructor
(B) What happens in the weeks after the training, especially supervisor expectations
(C) The length of the course
(D) The number of participants
206. Why does the passage call the finding "inconvenient"?
(A) It requires more budget.
(B) It places responsibility outside the training department.
(C) It contradicts earlier research.
(D) It applies only to large organizations.
207. What does the passage say about sending someone to a course they cannot use?
(A) It is better than sending no one.
(B) It is worse than sending no one.
(C) It has no effect either way.
(D) It should be decided by the participant.
看詳解與全文中譯
📋 答案：203.(B)　204.(C)　205.(B)　206.(B)　207.(B)
題答案解析
203
細節題		(B)首句：describe training as an investment, and most measure it as an expense——「說是投資、算成費用」正是那個 gap
204
細節題		(C)第 2 段：neither tells us whether anyone works differently afterwards
205
細節題		(B)第 3 段：what happens in the weeks following the training；並指出最強的預測因子是直屬主管是否期待他們使用
206
推論題		(B)第 4 段：it locates responsibility somewhere other than the training department——「不方便」是因為責任落到別人身上
207
細節題		(B)末段：sending someone to a course they cannot use is worse than sending no one——原句照抄
📖 全文中譯 　　多數組織把訓練描述為一種投資，卻把它衡量成一筆費用。這兩句話之間的落差，相當程度上解釋了為什麼訓練方案往往令人失望。

　　看看典型的方案是怎麼被評估的：記錄出席率、課程結束時發一張滿意度問卷，然後把結果以百分比呈報。這兩項指標都很容易蒐集，卻沒有一項告訴我們：課後有沒有任何人的工作方式因此改變。一門課可以很有趣、出席率很高，而且完全沒有效果。

　　關於職場學習的研究，一致指出另一項因素才是決定性的：訓練結束後那幾週發生了什麼事。短期內沒有被使用的技能大多會流失，而能否被使用，最強的預測因子是：參與者的直屬主管是否期待他去使用。一位在兩週後開口詢問「那個新方法用起來如何」的主管，把一門課變成了一項做法；一位從未提起的主管，則把它變成了一段回憶。

　　這項發現令人不便，因為它把責任放在訓練單位以外的地方。它意味著：改善訓練，或許與「開更好的課」關係不大，反而與「事先準備好主管」關係較大——事前讓主管知道部屬將學到什麼，並給他一個事後追蹤的理由。

　　還有一項進一步的推論。如果應用取決於機會，那麼把一個人送去上他用不到的課，比誰都不送更糟：它消耗預算、占用時間，還教會參與者一件事——訓練是拿來忍耐的，不是拿來應用的。因此，挑選正確的參訓對象並不是行政細節，而是一項實質的決定，值得與「選哪門課」得到同樣多的關注。
📝 值得抄進單字本的字 disappoint 令人失望｜attendance 出席｜distribute 發放｜decisive 決定性的｜predictor 預測因子｜immediate supervisor 直屬主管｜inconvenient 不便的｜locate responsibility 歸屬責任｜endure 忍耐｜substantive 實質的
💡 這篇直接對應你的公文範例 公文範例 23（派員參訓簽）的「擬辦二」——返所後 2 週內完成心得報告、並於所務會報經驗分享。
那正是本文所說的「事後追蹤」機制。寫那份簽的時候，你已經在做這篇文章建議的事了。
閱讀 14資料品質 · 208～211
　　Discussions of data usually focus on quantity. Agencies describe how many records they hold, how many transactions they process, how many datasets they have released. Quantity is visible and easy to report. Quality is neither.

　　Yet quality determines whether the quantity is worth anything. A register in which ten per cent of addresses are out of date will misdirect one in ten notices, and no amount of additional records will correct that. Worse, errors of this kind are usually invisible from inside the system: the notice is sent, the record shows it was sent, and only the citizen who never received it knows otherwise.

　　Practitioners distinguish several dimensions of quality, of which accuracy is only one. Completeness asks whether required fields are populated; timeliness asks whether the record reflects the present rather than the past; consistency asks whether the same person is recorded identically across systems. A dataset can score well on one dimension and poorly on another, and improving one sometimes worsens another — collecting more fields, for instance, increases completeness while creating more opportunities for inconsistency.

　　What makes data quality genuinely difficult is that it is rarely anyone's job. The unit that collects a record often does not use it; the unit that uses it cannot correct it. Responsibility is therefore distributed so thinly that no one experiences the consequences of poor quality directly, and the problem persists not because it is technically hard but because it is organizationally unowned.

　　The remedy most often proposed is to assign a "data steward" for each significant dataset — a named person accountable for its accuracy and for resolving disputes about it. The role sounds bureaucratic, and sometimes is. But it addresses the actual cause, which is not ignorance of what good data looks like, but the absence of anyone whose responsibility it is to produce it.
208. Why do agencies tend to focus on quantity?
(A) Quantity matters more than quality.
(B) Quantity is visible and easy to report.
(C) Quality cannot be measured at all.
(D) Regulations require it.
209. Why are address errors described as "invisible from inside the system"?
(A) The system deletes them automatically.
(B) The record shows the notice was sent, so only the citizen knows it was not received.
(C) Staff are not allowed to view addresses.
(D) The errors are encrypted.

(A) Technical difficulty
(B) Lack of funding
(C) That responsibility is distributed so thinly that it is organizationally unowned
(D) Insufficient staff training

(C) It should replace all technical solutions.
(D) It is impossible to implement.

📖 全文中譯 　　關於資料的討論，通常聚焦在數量：機關描述自己保有多少筆紀錄、處理多少交易、釋出了多少資料集。數量看得見，也容易呈報；品質兩者都不是。

　　然而，正是品質決定了那些數量值不值錢。一份地址有一成過時的名冊，會使每十件通知就有一件寄錯，而再多的新增紀錄也修正不了這一點。更糟的是，這類錯誤從系統內部通常是看不見的：通知寄出了，紀錄顯示已寄出，而唯一知道沒收到的人，是那位民眾。

　　實務工作者區分出品質的若干面向，正確性只是其中之一。完整性問的是必填欄位是否都有值；時效性問的是紀錄反映的是現在還是過去；一致性問的是同一個人在各系統中是否被記載得一模一樣。一份資料集可能在某個面向表現良好、在另一個面向很差，而改善其中一項有時會惡化另一項——例如蒐集更多欄位提高了完整性，同時卻製造出更多不一致的機會。

　　使資料品質真正困難的，是它幾乎不是任何人的工作。蒐集紀錄的單位往往不使用它；使用它的單位又無法更正它。責任因而被分散得極為稀薄，以致沒有人直接承受品質不良的後果；問題之所以持續存在，不是因為技術上困難，而是因為在組織上無人擁有。

　　最常被提出的解方，是為每一份重要資料集指定一位「資料管家」——一位具名、對其正確性負責、並負責解決相關爭議的人。這個角色聽起來很官僚，有時候也確實如此。但它處理的是真正的病因：問題不在於沒有人知道好資料長什麼樣子，而在於沒有人的職責是把它做出來。
📝 值得抄進單字本的字 register 名冊｜misdirect 寄錯｜out of date 過時｜dimension 面向｜completeness 完整性｜timeliness 時效性｜consistency 一致性｜populate（欄位）填入值｜distributed thinly 分散稀薄｜unowned 無人擁有｜steward 管家、託管者｜accountable for 對…負責
💡 一魚兩吃 「完整性、時效性、一致性、正確性」正是資料庫與資訊管理的資料品質考點。
而「責任分散導致無人負責」的洞見，可以直接寫進國文作文「論責任」或「談公私之辨」。
閱讀 15公共溝通 · 212～215
　　When an agency must announce bad news — a service failure, a data breach, a policy that will disadvantage some group — its instinct is usually to say as little as possible. The reasoning seems sound: fewer words, fewer things to be criticized for.

　　Communication researchers have found the opposite pattern in the way audiences respond. Vagueness does not reduce criticism; it invites speculation, and speculation is almost always less favourable than the facts. An announcement that "a technical issue affected some users" prompts readers to imagine the worst plausible version of events, and to conclude that the organization is concealing something. A statement specifying what failed, how many were affected, and what is being done tends to produce a narrower and more accurate reaction, even when the facts themselves are unflattering.

　　Timing works similarly. Organizations frequently delay announcements until an investigation is complete, reasoning that it is better to speak once with full information. But affected people rarely wait; they compare notes, and a version of events assembled from fragments circulates in the meantime. By the time the official account appears, it is competing with an established narrative rather than establishing one.

　　None of this argues for announcing conclusions before they are known. The recommended practice is to communicate early about what is known, what is not yet known, and when the next update will come — and then to provide that update on schedule, even if it contains little new information. The reliability of the schedule turns out to matter nearly as much as the content.

　　What makes this difficult is not that the principle is unclear but that following it requires accepting short-term discomfort — admitting uncertainty publicly — in exchange for a longer-term benefit that is harder to observe. Organizations that manage this trade-off well are rarely praised for it, because the crises they avoid leave no trace.

(A) To exaggerate the problem　(B) To say as little as possible
(C) To blame external parties　(D) To hold a press conference
213. According to researchers, what does vagueness produce?
(A) Less criticism
(B) Speculation, which is usually less favourable than the facts
(C) Complete indifference
(D) Faster resolution
214. What problem arises from delaying announcements?
(A) Investigations become more expensive.
(B) An unofficial narrative becomes established first.
(C) Staff lose interest.
(D) The law is violated.
215. Why are organizations that handle this well rarely praised?
(A) Their communication is too technical.
(B) The crises they avoid leave no trace.
(C) They refuse publicity.
(D) Their methods are secret.

(A) 正是被明確否定的

📖 全文中譯 　　當一個機關必須宣布壞消息時——服務中斷、資料外洩，或一項將使某些群體受損的政策——它的直覺通常是把話講得越少越好。這個推理聽起來合理：話說得少，可被挑剔的地方就少。

　　然而傳播研究者發現，閱聽者的反應模式恰好相反。含糊並不會減少批評，它招來的是揣測；而揣測幾乎總是比事實更不利。一則寫著「技術問題影響部分使用者」的公告，會促使讀者想像事件最壞的合理版本，並認定該組織正在隱瞞什麼。反之，一份明確指出「什麼壞了、多少人受影響、正在做什麼」的說明，往往能得到範圍較窄、也較準確的反應——即使那些事實本身並不好看。

　　時機的作用也類似。組織經常拖到調查完成才宣布，理由是「一次講清楚比較好」。但受影響的人很少會等；他們會彼此交換訊息，而一個由碎片拼湊起來的版本，在這段期間已經流傳開來。等到官方說法出現時，它面對的已不是「建立敘事」，而是「與一個既成的敘事競爭」。

　　以上並不是主張在結論尚未確定前就宣布結論。建議的做法是：及早溝通「已知什麼、還不知道什麼、下一次更新是什麼時候」——然後如期提供那次更新，即使它幾乎沒有新的內容。結果顯示，時程的可靠性，其重要性幾乎不亞於內容本身。

　　使這件事困難的，不是原則不清楚，而是遵循它必須接受短期的不適——公開承認不確定——以換取一個較難被觀察到的長期利益。能把這個取捨處理得好的組織，很少因此得到讚揚，因為他們所避免掉的危機，不會留下任何痕跡。
📝 值得抄進單字本的字 instinct 直覺｜vagueness 含糊｜speculation 揣測｜favourable 有利的｜plausible 貌似合理的｜conceal 隱瞞｜unflattering 不好看的｜narrative 敘事｜on schedule 如期｜trade-off 取捨｜leave no trace 不留痕跡
💡 這篇同時對應三處 ① 公文範例 15（陳報資安事件）：主動陳報、寫清楚時序與影響範圍
② 公文範例 26（事後檢討簽）：主動揭露「其他系統也有相同疏漏」
③ 資安申論：服務中斷應對外公告替代作業方式

「含糊招來揣測，而揣測總是比事實更不利」——這句話可以直接當作文金句。
閱讀 16制度設計 · 216～220（5 題）
　　Every rule is written with a typical case in mind. The difficulty is that rules are then applied to all cases, including those the drafter never imagined.

　　Two strategies exist for handling this. The first is to write rules in detail, anticipating as many situations as possible and specifying the treatment of each. The second is to write broad standards and rely on the judgment of the official applying them. Each has a characteristic failure. Detailed rules produce absurd outcomes when reality does not match the categories; broad standards produce inconsistency, because different officials reach different conclusions on similar facts.

　　Which failure is preferable depends on circumstances that are often left unexamined. Where cases are numerous and similar, and where consistency matters more than fit, detailed rules are usually better: the applicant benefits from predictability, and the official is protected from pressure to make exceptions. Where cases vary widely and the cost of a wrong outcome is high, standards allow the response to match the situation.

　　A third consideration is frequently decisive but rarely stated: the capability of those who will apply the rule. Standards demand judgment, and judgment requires training, experience, and time to deliberate. An organization that assigns broad discretion to inexperienced staff working under heavy caseloads has not chosen flexibility; it has chosen inconsistency while calling it flexibility.

　　The practical conclusion is that rule design and organizational capacity cannot be decided separately. A rule that would work well in one office may fail in another, not because the rule differs but because the conditions of its application do. This is uncomfortable for anyone who hopes to draft a rule once and apply it everywhere, which is to say for most rule-drafters — and it explains why so many well-written rules produce disappointing results in practice.
216. What basic difficulty does the first paragraph identify?
(A) Rules are written badly.
(B) Rules written for typical cases are applied to all cases.
(C) Officials do not read rules.
(D) Rules change too frequently.

(A) Inconsistency between officials
(B) Absurd outcomes when reality does not match the categories
(C) They take too long to write
(D) They are always too lenient

(B) When cases are numerous and similar, and consistency matters more than fit
(C) When staff are highly experienced

(B) It produces inconsistency while being called flexibility.
(C) It reduces workload.
(D) It is required by law.

(A) Rules should always be detailed.
(B) Rules should always be broad.
(C) Rule design and organizational capacity cannot be decided separately.
(D) Rules should be abolished.
